- Name check: `FaceRoles`, `face_roles`: 0 definitions; `.claude/` prose only (2 files each).

### 2.4 Transport: `orpheus/transport/boundary_conditions/` (new package; first commits of slice S2, not additive leaves)

`[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1 built this package as units L2, L3 (additive leaves) behind a transit exemption on the qualname gate; the user's ruling (thirteenth exchange; orchestrator decision C3) reverses the premise: no exemption (it would be a dual spelling under another name), so the package arrives in the sequential carve as a move, in S2, gates first (section 6.2, "S2a"). The module table keeps the unit letters only as pointers to DRAFT 1's reviewers.

| module | holds |
|---|---|
| `condition.py` | `BoundaryCondition` (the abstract base), `WallCondition` (the one shared exterior type and its facades) |
| `network.py` | the factors `MirrorPermutation`, `CurrentRetraction`, `IsotropicSection` (their tiers are numerics' `TraceRole`: no `Rung`), the closed chain sum `Chain = SpecularChain \| DiffuseChain`, `chain_composes`, the marker `ZeroNetwork`, `ResponseNetwork` |
| `inflow.py` | `InflowSampler`, `FaceFrameInflow`, `LabFrameInflow`, `InflowTerms` |
| `displacement.py` | `WallDisplacement` |
| `errors.py` | `BoundaryError`, `SubmarkovViolationError`, `BoundaryResponseNotPositiveError`, `BoundaryRefused`, `UnboundTagError`, `UnusedConditionError`, `NonInvariantConditionError` |
| `declared.py` | `BoundaryConditions` (the conditions declared for this problem, the twin of `Materials`) |
| `binding.py` | `BoundaryCarrier` (a Protocol), `Binding`, `bind` |
| `admission.py` | `Realized`, `Approximated` (`Approximated.of`), `Refused`, `RefusalAxis`, `Admission`, `FaceSite`, `SiteClass` (three geometric members), `StateCone` |
| `realization.py` | `RealizerTable`, `ResponseRealizer`, `SourceRealizer`, `DeckRealizer`, `sample_terms`, `stamp_boundary_role` |
| `__init__.py` | the re-exports; nothing else |

`transport/boundary_realization.py` retires into `realization.py` at WF2.4 (its `stamp_boundary_role` survives, `realize_recursively` and `BoundaryRealizer` retire; `[M]` `stamp_boundary_role` has 2 production importers, `_census_g24.out`). **No `RegistryMixin`, no `key=`, no `kind`, no `geometry_map`, no `response_kernel`, no welded `source.evaluate(space)`, no `assert_*(quadrature)`:** each was a weld of `BoundaryTraceLaw` (`orpheus/geometry/boundary/_base.py:144-581`): the string dispatch (ruling 17), the deck slot a response never fills non-trivially, the method's certification living on the declaration. (DRAFT 1 also removed `source` from the base. That was a second error: the affine part `q` is the translation half of the affine map, not a weld, and its removal left `q` no path through a combination. The base now carries `source` as an abstract ACCESSOR returning an `InflowTerms`, the leaf `WallCondition` stores it as its field `inflow` (decision 47), and the sampling stays the method's, decision 32.)

#### `network.py` (S2a): the response as a tensor network of typed factors

```python
# The tier marker is numerics' TraceRole (orpheus/numerics/spaces/angular_trace_space.py:168), widened by ONE member in leaf L4 (decision 49, section 2.5):
#   TraceRole = Literal["full", "outflow", "current", "inflow"]   # the four tiers of one face's Gamma ladder; "current" is `current_space` (already named angular_trace[<face>:current])
# network.py imports TraceRole; no second enumeration of the tiers (no `Rung`) exists.

@dataclass(frozen=True) class MirrorPermutation:    ...  # P.  no fields.  domain: ClassVar[TraceRole] = "outflow"; codomain: ClassVar[TraceRole] = "inflow"
@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurrentRetraction:    ...  # Rt. no fields.  "outflow" -> "current"   (the retraction of the split pair)
@dataclass(frozen=True) class IsotropicSection:     ...  # E.  no fields.  "current" -> "inflow"    (its section; divides by the INFLOW mass)
Factor: TypeAlias = MirrorPermutation | CurrentRetraction | IsotropicSection

@dataclass(frozen=True) class SpecularChain:   # s P
    factors: ClassVar = (MirrorPermutation,);                     unit_return: ClassVar[float] = 1.0
@dataclass(frozen=True) class DiffuseChain:    # d (E o Rt); listed in the order they are applied
    factors: ClassVar = (CurrentRetraction, IsotropicSection);    unit_return: ClassVar[float] = 1.0
Chain: TypeAlias = SpecularChain | DiffuseChain          # a CLOSED sum (decision 48): editing this alias is the only way to add a chain
CHAIN_ORDER: tuple[Chain, ...] = (SpecularChain(), DiffuseChain())          # the canonical order
def chain_composes(factors: tuple[type[Factor], ...]) -> bool    # the decidable composition parse: factors[0].domain == "outflow", factors[i].codomain == factors[i+1].domain, factors[-1].codomain == "inflow"
# module level, run at import: every member of get_args(Chain) satisfies chain_composes, else the module raises TypeError (an explicit raise, not an assert: the suite runs under python -O)
@dataclass(frozen=True) class ZeroNetwork: ...    # the marker of the empty network (R = 0): the key of the method's zero image

@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class ResponseNetwork(ContentIdentity):            # the linear part R as a weighted sum of chains, in canonical form
    weights: FrozenMapping[Chain, float]           # parsed: chain keys; finite reals (canonical_real: -0.0 is 0.0); a zero weight is not stored; order is not content
    @property cone_point -> tuple[float, float]    # (s, d): ONE exhaustive `match` over the chain of each weight (SpecularChain -> s, DiffuseChain -> d, `case _: assert_never(chain)`); 0.0 when absent. Total: the closed sum IS the pair, so the chart is not lossy (recheck_elegance B9)
    @property return_fraction -> float             # math.fsum(w * chain.unit_return)
    @property terms -> tuple[tuple[float, Chain], ...]   # in CHAIN_ORDER; the realizers' functor reads this
    __add__ (weights added chain by chain), scalar __mul__ (every weight multiplied by a float)
```
- **Typed factors, decidable composition.** A chain is a sequence of factors whose tiers compose, `"outflow"` to `"inflow"`; this is the predicate `chain_composes`, applied to every member of the closed sum when `network.py` is imported, so `(IsotropicSection, CurrentRetraction)` or a chain that ends on `"current"` is unspellable in the shipped module and refused by the predicate on a planted tuple (law L-M). The decidable criterion of `_factors.py:26-70` is the admission test for a NEW chain: a mirror-like chain is constitutive; an averaging chain is a kernel; neither is a deck.
- **The family is closed, and why (decision 48; `recheck_elegance.md` B4).** `[M]` `Chain: TypeAlias = A | B` is a `typing.Union`, which has no creation hook (`__init_subclass__`), so a check "when the class is created" could cover only the chains the module lists, and an open family would have been unenforceable. The family is therefore a closed sum like the decks: an exhaustive `match` with `assert_never` wherever a chain is read (`ResponseNetwork.cone_point`, `WallCondition.from_amplitudes`' builder), and a gate that `network.py` defines exactly the chain classes `get_args(Chain)` lists and no other class carrying a `factors` class attribute. A method-only kernel is another `BoundaryCondition` type with its own table entry (section 2.7), never a chain; the table's keys are the members of `get_args(Chain)`, `ZeroNetwork`, `InflowTerms` and the method-only condition types. `weights` is keyed by chain INSTANCES (field-less singletons) and the realizer table by chain TYPES: the functor looks up `type(chain)` and carries the weight beside it (type-keyed dispatch, instance-carried data), which stays correct the day a chain gains a parameter (recheck_elegance B8).
- **The unit return of a chain is declared and witnessed, not derived symbolically.** Deriving `rho(chain)` from its factors needs the trace measure, which the declaration does not hold; so each chain declares `unit_return` and law L-A's rider gates it on the typed image (section 2.6). Both shipped chains declare `1.0`.
- **The encoder takes it.** `[M]` `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_amend_chain_mapping.py`: a `ContentIdentity` holding a `FrozenMapping` keyed by field-less frozen dataclasses has equal digest, equality and hash under reordering of the entries and a different digest under a changed weight (4 of 4 comparisons True), so `weights` needs no new encoder rule.
- **The canonical form is the coordinates themselves.** No leaf collapse: DRAFT 1's rule that a one-term sum collapsed to a leaf storing a rounded partial sum is gone, so no stored value holds an intermediate rounding that a second addition rounds again.
- **Why a marker for the empty network.** Realization is a functor on the network (below), and the image of the empty sum is the method's zero map, which is method-specific (SN's `ZeroMap`, diffusion's zero scalar). The marker is the key under which a method registers it.
- **Name check (homonyms; AST over 1148 tracked `orpheus/` and `tests/` Python files; positive control `BoundaryTraceLaw` found once at `orpheus/geometry/boundary/_base.py:144`).** `[M]` `_amend_names.py`: `ResponseNetwork`, `MirrorPermutation`, `CurrentRetraction`, `IsotropicSection`, `SpecularChain`, `DiffuseChain` at 0 definitions. DRAFT 2 also cited `_amend_names.py` for `Rung`, `ZeroNetwork`, `ResponseRealizer`, `DeckRealizer`, `TableNotSealedError` and `SealedTableError`; `[M]` that script's `NAMES` lists none of those six (`recheck_elegance.md` B5), so the fact was true and the citation named the wrong instrument. The right instrument is `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_qa_d3_names_extended.py` (same AST predicate, same 1148 files): the six, and 30 more (`Factor`, `Chain`, `CHAIN_ORDER`, `InflowSampler`, `FaceFrameInflow`, `LabFrameInflow`, `Realized`, `Approximated`, `Refused`, `RefusalAxis`, `Admission`, the five transport errors, `realize_boundary`, `realize_boundary_role`, `roles_of_axes`, `FaceRoles`, `face_roles`, `conditions_of`, `ReferenceBoundaryKind`, `from_amplitudes`, `cone_point`, `return_fraction`, `sample_inflow`, ...) read 0 definitions each, with controls `BoundaryTraceLaw` 1, `TraceRole` 1, `stamp_boundary_role` 1. `[M]` `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_d3_names_sites.py` (DRAFT 3's new names `BoundaryCarrier`, `SourceRealizer`, `SNSource`, `DiffusionSource`, `sample_terms`, `chain_composes`): 0 definitions each, controls `TraceRole`, `stamp_boundary_role`, `PartialCurrentOperator`, `IsotropicEmissionOperator`, `ZeroMorphism`, `PermutationOperator` 1 each. `Rung` is not minted (decision 49): `TraceRole` is the tier marker. The factor names say the arrow (retraction and section per `axis_marginal.py`: "embedding" is not an operator name).

#### `condition.py` (S2a)

```python
class BoundaryCondition(ContentIdentity, ABC):       # the base of every exterior condition: the shared family and the method-only ones
    @property @abstractmethod
    def return_fraction(self) -> float                 # rho = J^-/J^+ of the linear part; the DECLARATION's closed form (a ZeroFluxCondition reports -1.0)
    @property @abstractmethod
    def source(self) -> "InflowTerms"                  # the affine part q; empty for a condition with none. Read by `Binding.sources` for EVERY condition type (no isinstance on a leaf, C1). The base's contract is this ACCESSOR: each leaf spells its own storage (decision 47)
    @property @abstractmethod
    def face_local(self) -> bool                       # stated in the face's own frame, so every isometry carries it from face to face
    def invariant_under(self, isometry: "FaceIsometry") -> bool    # concrete: self.face_local or isometry.is_identity.  FaceIsometry is imported under TYPE_CHECKING only

@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class WallCondition(BoundaryCondition):              # the ONE shared exterior type: the affine pair (R, q)
    network: ResponseNetwork     # parsed (cone): every weight >= 0 (BoundaryResponseNotPositiveError); math.fsum(w * unit_return) <= 1.0 (SubmarkovViolationError)
    inflow: InflowTerms = field(default_factory=InflowTerms.empty)   # parsed: every weight >= 0 (q in the positive cone). The FIELD is named `inflow` and `source` is a property returning it (decision 47): a dataclass field named `source` under the base's abstract property cannot be instantiated
    @property source -> InflowTerms                       # = self.inflow; satisfies the base's abstract accessor
    # the cone is ONE definition: `_cone_refusal(network, inflow) -> BoundaryError | None` (the predicate of decision 50). `__post_init__` raises what it returns; the arithmetic below turns what it returns into a `WallDisplacement`
    # facades (classmethods; each returns an EQUAL WallCondition, never a second type):
    @classmethod vacuum() -> "WallCondition"                         # the zero network, no source
    @classmethod specular(cls, albedo: float = 1.0) -> "WallCondition"   # albedo * P
    @classmethod white(cls, albedo: float = 1.0) -> "WallCondition"      # albedo * (E o Rt); D1's AlbedoBoundary.white, first positional argument the albedo
    @classmethod inflow(cls, value: float) -> "WallCondition"            # the zero network over a uniform q = value
    @classmethod framed(cls, profile: "FaceFrameInflow | LabFrameInflow", weight: float = 1.0) -> "WallCondition"   # the zero network over a sampled q
    @classmethod from_amplitudes(cls, specular: float, diffuse: float) -> "WallCondition"   # the plain-data edge, section 2.9; the section of cone_point
    @property cone_point -> tuple[float, float]          # (s, d) of the network: C12's verb, so a test never rebuilds the chart by isinstance
    # return_fraction = network.return_fraction;  face_local = not any lab-frame term in source (the network is face-local by construction)
    # operators: __add__, __radd__, __sub__, __rsub__, __neg__, scalar __mul__/__rmul__ ALL compute the pair (network, inflow) of the result and exit through ONE function,
    #            `_combine(network, inflow) -> WallCondition | WallDisplacement`: a WallCondition iff `_cone_refusal` is None, else a WallDisplacement (decision 50). The sign of a scalar and the presence of `-` decide nothing.
    #            WallDisplacement defines the same operators, so a chain of arithmetic needs no narrowing; the one narrowing site is the binding door (`BoundaryConditions` refuses a displacement).   NO __truediv__ (no consumer; division would leave the dyadic coordinates)
```
- **`albedo` and the cone parse (rulings D1, D2, adopted point 4).** The bounds move from SN's realization (`assert_submarkov`, `orpheus/geometry/boundary/albedo.py:201-214`) to construction: `WallCondition.specular(1.2)` raises `SubmarkovViolationError`, `.white(-0.1)` raises `BoundaryResponseNotPositiveError`. `[M]` `_probe_admission.py` showed diffusion realizing an albedo of `5.0` as `k = 2.972` (slab), `-4.015` (cylinder), `-1.111` (sphere) while SN refuses it. The cone check is the float sum `math.fsum(w * unit_return) <= 1.0`, correctly rounded; `[M]` `math.fsum([0.9, 0.1]) <= 1.0` is True and `math.fsum([1.0, 2**-52]) <= 1.0` is False, so an intended unit wall written as `0.9` plus `0.1` is accepted and a true excess of one double step is refused. `[R]` At the exact boundary two associations of one sum may differ by a rounding, one a condition and one a displacement (the bind door then refuses the latter); nothing in the tree builds a wall at `rho` within a few ulp of 1 other than by this arithmetic.
- **The twin is gone.** `albedo = 0` is admitted (18 test sites construct `AlbedoBoundary(0, ...)`, the albedo is a #529 parameter coordinate) and it IS the vacuum: `WallCondition.specular(0.0) == WallCondition.white(0.0) == WallCondition.vacuum()`, one digest, and the realized map is the method's zero image because the empty network is registered as `ZeroNetwork`. `tests/gates/geometry/test_boundary.py:663` ("`AlbedoBoundary(0, closure)` and vacuum are the same object" at the REALIZED map) is now a fact about the declaration too. Whether a face is vacuum is still answered by the REALIZED type (the `ZeroMap`), which now agrees with the declaration without an extra rule (attack elegance A1).
- **Face-local, no orientation field anywhere.** `WallCondition` and the factors hold no `axis` and no `outward_sign`; the realizer reads the face's normal from the face name (`face_normal(face)`, one parse). One condition object is then correct on every face of its tag, which a tag-keyed binding requires. `[M]` today the orientation is stated: `WhiteBoundary(axis, outward_sign)`, `SpecularReturn(axis)`, `IsotropicReturn(axis, outward_sign)` (`orpheus/geometry/boundary/_factors.py:1143-1416`, `white.py:110-112`), and `_law_from_tag` patches them per face (`orpheus/transport/method.py:352-407`) with a realizer cross-check catching a mismatch (B3.4a). Sizing `[M]` `_census_g24.out`: `SpecularReturn` 97 test constructions plus 1 production, `IsotropicReturn` 29 plus 0, `WhiteBoundary` 88 plus 2 (calls); each loses its orientation arguments.
- **`face_local` implies invariant only in one dimension (structure 6).** On a Cartesian slab the colouring group acts on faces by the mirror `x -> a + b - x`, which has no tangential coordinate to reverse, so a face-local condition is invariant. In higher dimension a mirror that maps a face to itself reverses or permutes the face's tangent frame, so a `FaceFrameInflow` that reads a tangential coordinate would not be invariant. Not live (a `Mesh2D` carries `UncomputedSymmetries`, where `bind` refuses every non-face-local condition on a multi-face tag and states the missing machinery); the dimension quantifier travels with `face_local`'s docstring and with law L-C.
- **`WhiteBoundary` retires into `WallCondition.white(alpha)` (D1).** `[M]` `WhiteBoundary.response_kernel` builds exactly the kernel `AlbedoBoundary(alpha, IsotropicReturn)` builds (`white.py:147-160`), and `_probe_admission_num.out` shows SN `WhiteBoundary(0.5)` and `AlbedoBoundary(0.5, isotropic)` agree bitwise on the slab (k = 0.53578). The old `WhiteBoundary(0.4)` took `axis` first: `white`'s first positional argument is the albedo. The kernel tier (`ScalarResponse`, `LambertianReemission`, `SpecularReemission`, `ReemissionClosure`, `BoundaryResponseKernel`) and the closures `SpecularReturn`, `IsotropicReturn` fold into the chains: they have no content beyond `(alpha, shape)` once the conditions are face-local, and moving them would carry a tier duplicating the network (X4).
- **Name check (`_amend_names.py`, same instrument as above).** `WallCondition`: 0 definitions. The strongest claim on "wall" is the reference's `Wall(breakpoint, specular, diffuse, partner)` (`derivations/continuous/characteristic/walls.py:77`), a POSITIONED wall of the reference; the production type is a face's exterior condition and takes the suffix of its base, `WallCondition`, which is a different identifier. `BoundaryCondition`: the production base wins; the `Literal` alias `orpheus/derivations/common/continuous_reference.py:72` (read by `ProblemSpec.boundary_conditions`) cedes and is renamed `ReferenceBoundaryKind`, and the field is renamed with it, `boundary_kinds` (section 7 row R18, slice S5; C10). The two condition TYPES end in `Condition` (`WallCondition`, `ZeroFluxCondition`) and no retiring class does (the old ones end in `Boundary`, `Inflow` or `Return`); every other new name is fresh by the AST pass, so no new class shares a qualname with an old one (decision 30).

#### `inflow.py` (S2a)

```python
class InflowSampler(Protocol):                     # the open protocol; the existing `InflowSourceSpec.evaluate(space)` renamed
    def sample(self, space: FunctionSpace) -> np.ndarray     # q on `space` with exactly space.shape, in the units of psi (NOT W-normalised)

@dataclass(frozen=True) class FaceFrameInflow:  sampler: InflowSampler    # written in the face's own frame (mu = Omega.n_f and tangential position); face_local True
@dataclass(frozen=True) class LabFrameInflow:   sampler: InflowSampler    # written in laboratory directions; face_local False
InflowProfile: TypeAlias = FaceFrameInflow | LabFrameInflow

@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class InflowTerms(ContentIdentity):                 # q as a weighted sum of profiles: the affine part of a condition. A ContentIdentity like every other value (no custom dunders; roster entry in S2a gate 15)
    uniform: float                                  # the total weight on a uniform profile: every uniform contribution folds into this one number (c * inflow(v) contributes c * v)
    sampled: tuple[tuple[float, InflowProfile], ...]    # (weight, open profile), in first-appearance order; a zero weight is not stored (the drop rule of `ResponseNetwork.weights`, decision 50), so `0.0 * WallCondition.framed(f)` holds the empty inflow and equals `WallCondition.vacuum()`. The profiles are contentless, so a source holding one has IDENTITY equality and its digest raises ContentlessError (as Binding's does, and as 2.10's `_law_key` fallback already handles); the uniform-only case is ordinary content
    @classmethod empty();  __add__, scalar __mul__;  @property is_zero (= uniform == 0.0 and not sampled), face_local
```
- **The frame is the type, the content is open.** `[M]` the three tree implementors of `evaluate(space)` are Python callables over the space, not closed expressions: `_ManufacturedFaceInflow` (`tests/gates/sn/verification/analytical/test_mms_declared_inflow.py:125`, reads `space.directions` and evaluates `(A + mu B)/W` with per-face coefficients), `_Liar` (`tests/gates/transport/test_boundary_source_from_specs.py:144`) and `_Ramp` (`:217`, row order `q[n, g] = n + 10 g`, deliberately). So no closed `Symbolic` profile union is possible, and the frame type is what ruling 2 needs: only a `LabFrameInflow` term owes an invariance check (attack elegance B5, math 4). `UniformInflow` of DRAFT 1 is gone: a uniform inflow is the number `uniform`, folded, so `inflow(1.0) + inflow(2.0) == inflow(3.0)` (structure frame 1's first test) without a profile object for a constant.
- **`invariant_under(h)` of a lab-frame term is conservative and deterministic:** `h.is_identity`. A lab-frame profile is an open callable, `function o h == function` is undecidable for it, and sampling it on the two faces' nodes at a tolerance (design B) is a check keyed on nothing numerical. The consequence: a condition with a lab-frame term on a tag whose faces are moved by a non-identity element of `H` is refused; on a one-face tag, an asymmetric slab, two tags or a uniform or face-frame source it binds. This reproduces the authored first red of gate G12 (design B) with no tolerance. Trigger for a decision procedure: a closed (`Symbolic`) lab-frame profile. Ruling 2's phrase "only the method can see that" is true of equivariance (which `ordinate_pairing` certifies) and not of invariance of a declared profile, which is the declaration's (attack math 4): a sharpening reported in section 9 (decision 10, confirmed), not a contradiction.
- **Contentless profiles.** A callable has no content. `FaceFrameInflow` and `LabFrameInflow` are `ContentIdentity`-free frozen dataclasses keyed by object, exactly as the three implementors are today (`_law_key` in `orpheus/transport/mesh/material_mesh.py:81` keys an unhashable law by `(qualname, id)`); `content_digest` of a geometry-free binding holding a condition whose `sampled` is non-empty raises `ContentlessError` (DRAFT 1's second leg of finding V1, the digest sort that raised on an open sampler, is closed because nothing sorts by digest: sampled terms stay in first-appearance order), and a `GeometrySpecification` cannot hold one (it states plain data, section 2.9).
- **`InflowSourceSpec.evaluate(space)` was a realization welded onto the declaration** (`orpheus/geometry/boundary/_source.py:119`, `item2_carve.md`); sampling is the method's side and has ONE verb, `RealizerTable.sample_inflow(terms, site, space)` (the door: `require`, then the registered `SourceRealizer.sample`), whose default body is the module function `sample_terms(terms, space)` in `realization.py` (`transport` may name a numerics `FunctionSpace`): `q = uniform * ones + sum_i w_i sampler_i.sample(space)` (decision 53). `NoSource` retires: an inflow of zero is `InflowTerms.empty()`. `[M]` `NoSource` calls: prod 2, tests 12; `ConstantInflowSource` calls: tests 53 in 18 files.

#### the algebra, and `displacement.py` (S2a)

The algebra on declarations, per D2, is the algebra of the one type, with the cone as a VALUE predicate (decision 50, `orchestrator, DRAFT 3`; `recheck_structure.md` frame 4): `+`, `-`, unary `-` and scalar `*` add the networks chain by chain and the sources term by term (the zero-weight drop reaching both), and the result is a `WallCondition` iff `_cone_refusal` finds nothing (every network weight `>= 0`, `math.fsum(w * unit_return) <= 1.0`, every inflow weight `>= 0`), else a `WallDisplacement`. Direct construction of a `WallCondition` outside the cone raises, and so do the facades (`specular(1.2)`, `white(-0.1)`). `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 2's three spelling rules (`c >= 0` gives a condition and raises outside `K`; `c < 0` gives a displacement; `-` always gives a displacement), which decided the return type by the scalar's sign and gave three behaviours for one event (`recheck_elegance.md` B6, `recheck_structure.md` frame 4); the fact the value predicate establishes: what cannot be bound is a value outside `K`, however it was spelled, and `x + h * delta` for #529's finite differences re-enters `K` without re-typing. There is no separate normal-form function, no `CombinedBoundary` and no collapse rule: the canonical form IS the coordinates (decision 28; `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1's `normal_form(terms, cone=)` and `CombinedBoundary`, which carried the two defects of both checks: a rewrite of `AlbedoBoundary(a, s)` as weight `c a` on a unit (a type-specific branch inside the shared body, elegance C12) and a collapse to a leaf that stored a rounded partial sum, structure frame 2).
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class WallDisplacement(ContentIdentity):           # a combination OUTSIDE the cone: a displacement in the affine hull of K x (positive source cone). NOT a BoundaryCondition. Never bindable.
    network: ResponseNetwork                       # signed weights allowed
    inflow: InflowTerms                            # signed weights allowed (same field name as WallCondition: `_combine` builds either from one pair)
    @classmethod def of(cls, *terms: tuple[float, WallCondition]) -> "WallDisplacement"    # the displacement constructor: ALWAYS a displacement, even when the combination lies in K (then it is still unbindable and unequal to the condition of the same coordinates)
    # closed under +, -, scalar *;  WallCondition + WallDisplacement is `_combine` of the summed pair (a condition iff the sum re-enters K)
```
- **Properties of the result, each a gate, each with its quantifier (structure 2).** Write `x` for an admitted `WallCondition`.
  - exact on every value whose source is uniform-only (the content case): `a + b == b + a` (float addition is commutative per coordinate). A source holding a sampled term has identity equality (it is contentless), so `a + b` and `b + a` are two objects and the law is not stated for it; the open-sampler cases are gated on what they CAN state (no raise, no lost weight, `ContentlessError` on the digest); `WallCondition.vacuum() + x == x` (adding `0.0` is exact); `1.0 * x == x`; `0.0 * x == WallCondition.vacuum()` (a zero weight is dropped from the network AND from `InflowTerms.sampled`, `-0.0` is canonical `0.0`; this one holds on EVERY admitted `x`, a sampled source included, because the result holds no profile and is content-full: `[M]` `_d3_cone_arith.py` G5, control `no-drop` False); `0.5 * WallCondition.specular(1.0) == WallCondition.specular(0.5)` (a power of two scales exactly); `WallCondition.specular(0.25) + WallCondition.specular(0.5) == WallCondition.specular(0.75)` (a dyadic sum is exact).
  - `(a + b) + c == a + (b + c)`: exact when every partial sum of every coordinate is exactly representable (dyadic coordinates of small bit-width, the fixture `0.25, 0.5, 0.125`); for general doubles the two sides agree coordinate by coordinate up to ONE rounding of the sum, so two spellings of one physical wall can differ in digest (a cache MISS, never a wrong answer). `[M]` `(0.1 + 0.2) + 0.3 = 0.6000000000000001` and `0.1 + (0.2 + 0.3) = 0.6` (`_amend_fraction_content.py`); the gate states the dyadic leg as exact and the `0.1, 0.2, 0.3` leg at one ulp. DRAFT 1 gated both as exact and the non-dyadic triple reddened it (structure check, premise 1: 2 of the 7 listed identities failed on admitted inputs). `[REFUTED 2026-10-22, FOR THE QUESTION "make associativity exact on every double"]` exact rational coordinates (`fractions.Fraction`): the content encoder refuses a `Fraction` (`ContentlessError`, `[M]` `_amend_fraction_content.py`) and a rational coordinate type is a new value; the fact it establishes: the encoder takes tuples of ints, so a `(numerator, denominator)` pair would carry content. Trigger to revisit: a content-keyed cache hit across re-associated sums that #529 needs.
  - `return_fraction(a + b) == return_fraction(a) + return_fraction(b)` on dyadic coordinates; otherwise up to one rounding (the right side is two roundings of a sum of three or four terms).
- **The value predicate, its gates and its measurement.** `[M]` `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_d3_cone_arith.py` (a toy of the coordinates `(s, d, uniform, sampled)` with exactly these rules; it measures that the rules are consistent, and imports no tree type): `specular(0.5) + (white(0.3) - white(0.3)) == specular(0.5)` and is a condition (G1; under DRAFT 2's spelling it was a displacement); `2.0 * specular(0.6)` is a displacement (G2; the control, DRAFT 2's sign rule, RAISES on it, so G2 discriminates the two rules); `specular(0.5) - specular(0.3)` is a condition (G3); `white(0.3) - specular(0.5)` and `0.6 * specular + 0.6 * white` are displacements (G4, G6); `0.0 * framed(f) == vacuum()` (G5; control: a copy that keeps `(0.0, f)` in `sampled` reads False); a displacement plus its inverse step re-enters `K` as a condition (G7); `specular(1.2)`, `white(-0.1)` and a direct two-chain `(0.6, 0.6)` raise (G8, 3 of 3); `specular(0.9) + white(0.1)` is a condition by the float cone sum (G9). 13 of 13 lines read as stated. The cost the predicate carries, said once: the return type of arithmetic is `WallCondition | WallDisplacement` by value, which `recheck_elegance.md` B6 named as a repeated conditional in consumers; it is answered by the displacement type defining the same operators (no narrowing is needed to keep computing) and by the single narrowing site at the binding door, so no consumer branches on the sort.
- **Operators.** `+`, `-`, unary `-` and scalar `*` return `_combine(...)`: a `WallCondition` iff the result lies in `K`, else a `WallDisplacement`. A method-only condition defines no operator (`ZeroFluxCondition() + x` is Python's own `TypeError`), and neither does a deck: `0.5 * MirrorDeck()` is Python's own `TypeError: unsupported operand` (law L-F; this retires the `ELEGANCE-DEBT[guard] #551` at `_composition.py:113-141` with its tag, and `Closes #551` rides the WF2.4 commit after `gh issue view 551` confirms the title).
- **The two existing tests that pin a negative coefficient** (`tests/gates/geometry/test_law_composition.py:165-186`, `spec - white` and `-s`) are re-posed on `WallDisplacement.of`, which is a displacement whatever the operands (`[R]` under the value predicate the arithmetic spellings `spec - white` and `-s` also leave `K` for the operands those tests use; S2a gate 6 reads the operands and uses `.of` so the pin does not depend on that). `[M]` `LawSum` calls: prod 12 in 2 files, tests 12 in 2 files (`_census_g24.out`); no production binding exists (`parse_boundary_law` refuses a composite, `structured_geometry.py:547`).

#### `errors.py` (S2a)

```python
class BoundaryError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, declaration: object = None)    # `.declaration`: the object or its type; replaces the string `.law`
class SubmarkovViolationError(BoundaryError): ...                    # names kept (two message vocabularies survive; retirement-audit B.11)
class BoundaryResponseNotPositiveError(BoundaryError): ...
class BoundaryRefused(BoundaryError):    method: str; face: str; admission: Refused
class UnboundTagError(BoundaryError):    tag: int; face: str
class UnusedConditionError(BoundaryError): tag: int
class NonInvariantConditionError(BoundaryError): tag: int; isometry: FaceIsometry; faces: tuple[str, ...]
```
- `BoundaryError.law: str` (`orpheus/geometry/boundary/_errors.py:63`) becomes `.declaration`. `[M]` 39 `err.law` comparisons in tests (design B) and 12 pins (`item3_strings.md` (e)); both re-pose in the slice of their test file (`isinstance(err.declaration, ...)`, `err.declaration == MirrorDeck()`). `BoundaryError` is transport's, geometry has `DeckError`, numerics has `OrdinatePairingError` (section 2.6): the eleventh exchange says geometry holds no module named for boundary conditions, so the census's "rooted in geometry" is replaced (item 2 of `item2_carve.md`).
- The six certification errors of `geometry/boundary/_errors.py` (`IncomingOutgoingTraceClassification...Error`, `VacuumAppliedToOutgoingTraceError` [ERR-041], `BoundaryGeometryMapNotMeasurePreservingError` [ERR-042], `ReflectionNotInvolutiveError` [ERR-044], `ReflectionDidNotMapInflowToOutflowError` [ERR-045], `BoundarySourceNotOnIncomingTraceError` [ERR-047]) leave with the certification. The three of ERR-042, 044 and 045 become the numerics errors (section 2.5). The other three check the SN realizer's OWN index sets (the inflow/outflow classification of the trace it builds, the support of the sampled `q`), which the type system does not replace: they stay SN-side assertions in a new `orpheus/sn/boundary/errors.py` rooted in `BoundaryError` (slice S2), and their catchers (`sn/operators/test_sn_boundary_realizer.py:1180` for ERR-041, `transport/test_boundary_source_from_specs.py:60` for ERR-047) keep their paths. A test that asserts a certification failure through `pytest.raises(BoundaryError)` re-points to the numerics type; `[M]` 37 `raises(BoundaryError)` sites in 23 files (design B), each read by hand.

#### `declared.py` (S2a) and `binding.py` (S2a)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class BoundaryConditions(ContentIdentity):                          # declared.py: the exact twin of Materials (orpheus/data/materials.py:72)
    conditions: Mapping[int, BoundaryCondition]                    # stored as FrozenMapping in declared order
    # parsed: keys -> int (a bool is refused, TypeError); every value a BoundaryCondition (a WallDisplacement, a deck, a str is refused, TypeError naming the type)
    # ONE difference from Materials: EMPTY IS ADMITTED (a carrier of decks only, such as pwr_slab_half_cell, has no tag)
    @classmethod empty() -> BoundaryConditions;  @classmethod of(conds | Mapping) -> BoundaryConditions     # parse-at-boundary coercion
    __getitem__, __iter__, __len__, __contains__, keys, values, items, get; @property tags -> frozenset[int]

@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class Binding(ContentIdentity):                                      # binding.py
    decks: FrozenMapping[str, DeckTransformation]                  # faces glued to a partner or a mirror plane
    tags: FrozenMapping[str, int]                                  # the tag of each face facing the exterior
    declared: BoundaryConditions                                   # the conditions declared for this problem. The face-to-condition join is DERIVED from (tags, declared), never stored (C3)
    symmetry: Symmetries
    # invariant (parsed): decks and tags have disjoint keys; set(tags.values()) == declared.tags EXACTLY, so a Binding that joins a face to a condition of another tag cannot be built (DRAFT 1 stored `conditions: face -> condition` beside `tags`, and `Binding(tags={"xmax": 1}, conditions={"xmax": WallCondition.vacuum()}, ...)` where tag 1 was declared an albedo was spellable: elegance C3)
    @property faces -> tuple[str, ...]                             # inventory order (decks and tagged faces merged)
    def condition_of(self, face: str) -> BoundaryCondition         # declared[tags[face]]
    def faces_of(self, tag: int) -> tuple[str, ...]
    @property sources -> FrozenMapping[str, InflowTerms]           # the faces whose condition has a nonzero `source`, read through `BoundaryCondition.source` of EVERY condition (C1: no isinstance on a leaf), where the boundary source is read

class BoundaryCarrier(Protocol):                                  # the object that owns the faces and their colouring symmetry (decision 51)
    @property face_roles -> FaceRoles                            # the face inventory with each face's role (a deck or a tag)
    @property symmetry -> Symmetries                             # the colouring group of THE SAME faces (geometry's `colouring_symmetry` of this carrier)

def bind(carrier: BoundaryCarrier, conditions: BoundaryConditions | Mapping[int, BoundaryCondition]) -> Binding
    # reads `carrier.face_roles` and `carrier.symmetry` inside, once: a binding checked against another carrier's group is unspellable, and the fourteen heads no longer re-spell the pairing
```
- **`bind` takes the carrier, not its two derived values (decision 51; `recheck_elegance.md` B7).** `(roles, symmetry)` is a G-set (the group acting on the faces the roles label), so passing the two separately admitted `bind(roles_of(A), conditions, B.symmetry)`. `[M]` the heads hold three carrier kinds: `MaterialMesh` (SN: `orpheus/sn/problem.py`, diffusion: `orpheus/diffusion/augmented_mesh.py`) and `Mesh1D`/`Mesh2D` (CP `orpheus/cp/solver.py:203`, MoC `orpheus/moc/geometry.py:292`, MC `orpheus/mc/solver.py:144`); `Mesh1D` and `Mesh2D` already hold `face_roles` (after S1) and gain `symmetry` (S1), and `MaterialMesh` gains the property `face_roles` (S2b), which is `roles_of_axes(self.axes)` moved onto the object that owns the axes. A `bind` taking a `MaterialMesh` alone would not serve three of the five methods. **`bind` is the ONE shared body every method calls** (it replaces `resolve_boundary_conditions`, `orpheus/transport/method.py:225-260`, `_law_from_tag` `:271-420`, and the six string tables). It coerces `conditions` through `BoundaryConditions.of` once (so the fourteen method heads do not each coerce: elegance N16). It refuses, in this order: a tag a face carries with no condition (`UnboundTagError`, naming the face and the tag); a condition whose tag no face carries (`UnusedConditionError`; the exact cover catches a typo that a reachable-subset constructor would hide, attack elegance leaning-B; `BoundaryConditions` has no `restrict`: `[M]` `Materials.restrict` has 4 production callers, `_d3_names_sites.py`, and the spec names none for a conditions twin, so it would be dead weight, decision 56); a condition that is not invariant (`NonInvariantConditionError`): for every non-identity `h` of `symmetry`, every tag `t` whose faces `h` moves, `declared[t].invariant_under(h)` must hold; when `symmetry` is `UncomputedSymmetries`, any tag with two or more faces whose condition is not `face_local` is refused with a message naming the 2-D colouring group as the missing machinery (`SCOPE-BOUNDARY[guard]`). The `Binding` invariant above re-parses the exact cover, so the refusal text of `bind` (face-aware) and the parse of the value (structural) are the same fact stated at the door and at the type.
- **Reduce the group instead of refusing (ruling 2's alternative) is not built.** Nothing consumes the problem's group today (survey B), so a reduced group has no reader; the refusal names the reduction as the machinery that would admit it (`SCOPE-BOUNDARY[guard]`, machinery: a problem symmetry group consumed by a quotient or a cache, #529).
- **No value holds both families.** `Binding` keeps two typed maps (`decks`, `tags`) and the join is derived; the SN closure carries no `role` field (attack elegance B2). `SNProblem.reflective_axes` reads `binding.decks` (a declaration question, answered where the declaration lives).
- **`boundary_conditions` at the method heads.** Keyword-only, trailing, default `BoundaryConditions.empty()` `[CONFIRMED 2026-10-22, orchestrator C7, decision 6]`. The exact cover is the default's witness: a tagged carrier with no binding is refused loudly, naming each tag (S2b gate 12 builds a tagged SN problem with no `boundary_conditions` and requires `UnboundTagError`; its first red is the mutation that deletes the cover check in a copy of `bind`), a deck-only carrier needs nothing, and a required parameter would force 825 entry calls to pass `{}` where the carrier has no tag. Shape per ruling 6 of the eleventh exchange and ruling 9 of `layer_dag.md`: `SNProblem` is not renamed in P1 (480 sites), so the ruled `SNDiscretization(material_mesh, boundary_conditions)` lands as `SNProblem(mesh, quadrature, materials, ..., boundary_conditions=...)`; the rename is #522's. The head stores `self.binding`.
- **Name check.** `BoundaryConditions`, `Binding`, `bind`: 0 definitions (`_amend_names.py`). `.claude/` prose: `BoundaryConditions` 3 files. (`Binding` as a word occurs in prose; no class.)

#### `admission.py` (S2a)

```python
class RefusalAxis(Enum):
    ANGULAR_RESOLUTION       # can the method represent R's angular structure?
    TOPOLOGY                 # can its operator couple two faces (spatial / topological)?
    STATE_CONE               # is the value in the method's state cone (psi >= 0), or on its trace?
    PLUMBING                 # no theory refuses it: a missing registry entry or an unwired channel (the 'fourth thing')

@dataclass(frozen=True) class Realized:     faithful: bool                          # tier 1 True, tier 2 False
@dataclass(frozen=True) class Approximated: names: frozenset[str]; issues: frozenset[int]    # tier 3, and a named DOMAIN approximation (#547); a set because the meet of two takes the union. `Approximated.of(name: str, issue: int)` is the one-member spelling the tables write (decision 56)
@dataclass(frozen=True) class Refused:      axis: RefusalAxis; reason: str; issue: int | None = None
Admission: TypeAlias = Realized | Approximated | Refused
# THE LATTICE, defined once: Realized(True) > Realized(False) > Approximated > Refused.  meet(a, b) is the lesser; meet(Approximated x, Approximated y) = Approximated(x.names | y.names, x.issues | y.issues);
# meet of two Refused is the first argument's Refused (documented, deterministic); the meet of NO members is Realized(True) (an empty network and no source need no realizer). `Admission.meet(*members)` is the only spelling.

@dataclass(frozen=True) class FaceSite:                       # where a declaration is placed; derived from the carrier, computable before a method space exists
    face: str; coord: CoordSystem; dimension: int; hollow_inner: bool
    @property is_curved -> bool                               # coord is not CARTESIAN and not a Cartesian-axis face of a radial mesh
    @property site_class -> SiteClass                         # an Enum of the three GEOMETRIC classes of section 4.2 (CART, RAD_OUTER, RAD_INNER), derived once; the pin and the realizers read this name. CP's slab-left-face rule is CP's own predicate on `site.face` (decision 56)
```
- **Axes.** The three of the theory page plus `PLUMBING`. "Not built yet" is NOT an axis (adopted point 8): a refusal with no theory behind it is `PLUMBING` and carries the issue that tracks the missing machinery. This resolves the conflict between attack elegance S1 (a separate `NotBuilt` member) and adopted point 8 in favour of the ruling.
- **Members are participles.** `Exact` is taken (`orpheus/reference/certificate.py:105`, `[M]` `_defs.py`), `Realization` (`orpheus/geometry/symmetry.py:1291`) and `Refusal` (`orpheus/geometry/manifold.py:440`) too. `Realized`, `Approximated`, `Refused`, `Admission`, `RefusalAxis`, `FaceSite`: 0 class definitions; `Realized`, `Refused`, `Admission` occur as English words in 18, 21 and 20 files (docstrings and comments; `_homonyms.py`), which is not a clash.
- **Order and the upper bound.** The lattice above (elegance check, item 6 of "Not executable as written": DRAFT 1 had `Approximated.name: str` and a "meet" no section defined). A wall's admission is the meet over its nonzero chains and its source (section 2.4, "realization"); a sum of approximations carries the SET of their names (attack math 6: `meet` on a total order drops one name).

#### `realization.py` (S2a)

```python
class StateCone(Enum):                                                       # the cone the method's trace state lives in (C9: an Enum, not a bool that a forgetful method gets for free)
    NONNEGATIVE_FLUX                                                         # psi >= 0 on the trace: SN, CP, MoC, MC
    SIGNED_CURRENT                                                           # a signed partial current: diffusion

class RealizerTable(Generic[SpaceT, OpT]):
    def __init__(self, method: str, *, state_cone: StateCone)                # REQUIRED keyword
    def seal(self) -> None                                                   # called on the last line of the defining module; later registration raises
    def catalogue(self) -> Mapping[type, Admission]                          # the type-level UPPER BOUND over the table's keys, the front end's drop-down (before a problem exists)
    def admit(self, declared: DeckTransformation | BoundaryCondition, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT | None = None) -> Admission
        # ONE door, per face and per value. space=None: the type- and site-level answer (the front end; a face with no trace slot). space given: also the instance level that needs the method's discretization (SN: the quadrature's equivariance, via ordinate_pairing)
        # a WallCondition: Admission.meet over (admit(chain, weight) for its nonzero chains) + admit(ZeroNetwork) whenever the network is EMPTY, whatever the source (a pure inflow's linear image is the zero map, so the `ZeroNetwork` realizer is consulted for it) + admit(InflowTerms) if the source is nonzero
    def realize(self, declared: DeckTransformation | BoundaryCondition, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT) -> OpT   # calls admit(declared, site, space); raises BoundaryRefused iff it is Refused
        # a WallCondition: the FUNCTOR on its network: the image of the empty network is the ZeroNetwork realizer's; the image of one chain is that chain's realizer's; the image of a sum is the sum of the images (RealizedSum)
    def require(self, declared: DeckTransformation | BoundaryCondition, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT | None = None) -> None   # admit plus the raise of BoundaryRefused: the ONE definition of the refusal
    def sample_inflow(self, terms: InflowTerms, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT) -> np.ndarray   # the ONE source verb (decision 53): `require(terms, site, space)`, then the registered `SourceRealizer.sample`; the default body is the module function `sample_terms(terms, space)`: q = uniform * ones + sum_i w_i sampler_i.sample(space)

class ResponseRealizer(ABC, Generic[K, SpaceT, OpT]):
    status: ClassVar[Admission]                                              # the catalogue entry: the upper bound of admit over all values and sites
    def __init_subclass__(cls, *, table: RealizerTable, realizes: type[K], **kw)   # registers; refuses a duplicate, a sealed table, a key that is not a member of get_args(Chain), ZeroNetwork or a BoundaryCondition subclass (InflowTerms registers a SourceRealizer, below)
    def admit(self, unit: K, weight: float, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT | None = None) -> Admission   # default: self.status; value-, face- and discretization-dependent refusals override
    # `weight` is the chain's weight in the network; it is 1.0 for the ZeroNetwork key (which carries no scalar), and a method-only condition key is passed 1.0
    @abstractmethod
    def realize(self, unit: K, weight: float, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT) -> OpT

class SourceRealizer(ABC, Generic[SpaceT]):                                  # exactly one per table that admits a source: the realizer of the key `InflowTerms` (decision 53)
    status: ClassVar[Admission]
    def __init_subclass__(cls, *, table: RealizerTable, **kw)                # registers under the key InflowTerms; refuses a duplicate or a sealed table
    def admit(self, terms: InflowTerms, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT | None = None) -> Admission   # default: self.status
    def sample(self, terms: InflowTerms, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT) -> np.ndarray               # default: sample_terms(terms, space); SN overrides to run ERR-047's source-placement check on the sampled q
    # no `realize`, no OpT: a source is an array on the boundary-source channel, never an operator; a Refused entry (diffusion's) stubs nothing, because the door raises before `sample` is reached

class DeckRealizer(ABC, Generic[SpaceT, OpT]):                               # exactly one per table
    def __init_subclass__(cls, *, table: RealizerTable, **kw)
    @abstractmethod
    def admit(self, deck: DeckTransformation, pairing: FacePairing, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT | None = None) -> Admission     # one exhaustive match over DeckTransformation
    @abstractmethod
    def realize(self, deck: DeckTransformation, pairing: FacePairing, site: FaceSite, space: SpaceT) -> OpT
```
- **Keys.** The table's keys are the method's available units: the chain types `SpecularChain` and `DiffuseChain`, the marker `ZeroNetwork`, the marker `InflowTerms` (the source channel; its entry is a `SourceRealizer`, the others `ResponseRealizer`s) and the method-only condition types (`ZeroFluxCondition`). The chain keys are exactly the members of the closed sum `Chain` (decision 48). `WallCondition` itself is not a key: it is realized generically by the functor, so a method never writes a branch over the shared family's cone point. The ruling "the registry is per method, keyed by type, and filled by realizers" (eleventh exchange, 5) holds with the units as keys; a new chain type is one new realizer per method that admits it, and a method that does not register it refuses it through the unregistered-type door.
- **`realize_direction` is not minted.** `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1's `RealizerTable.realize_direction(direction, site, space)`: `[M]` it had 0 callers ("not reachable by bind"), its consumer is #529, and `dead_functions` would list it the day it lands. Trigger to mint: #529's first signed-sum consumer.
- **Registration (D, adopted 9, attack S2): a class keyword plus `seal()`.** `class SNSpecularChain(ResponseRealizer, table=SN_REALIZERS, realizes=SpecularChain)` registers at class creation. The table object and every realizer that registers into it are defined in ONE module per method and that module seals the table on its last line, so anyone holding the table has executed every registration (answers the registration-timing hazard of #290 P7b (#290 is CLOSED), an empty registry in a fresh process). An UNSEALED table refuses `admit`, `realize` and `catalogue` (`TableNotSealedError`). "A new response for one method touches only that method" holds: it touches that method's realizer module.
- **Exact-type lookup, never the MRO.** A subclass is a new declaration and owes its own entry; a method-only condition of another method must not be realized silently as its parent.
- **One door.** `realize` calls `admit` and raises `BoundaryRefused` (carrying the `Refused`) iff it is `Refused`; no realizer body raises on its own for an admission reason (attack A7: the query and the realization cannot disagree by design). The message is `f"{method} realizes {sorted(admitted type names)}; not {type(declared).__name__} ({axis}: {reason})"`. The one declared second door is POSING: the eigenvalue entries refuse a boundary source before any realization (`_refuse_a_boundary_source`, `orpheus/sn/solver.py:141,2294,2654`); that is a refusal of the QUESTION and not of the declaration, and law L-D names it.
- **An unregistered key.** `admit` returns `Refused(STATE_CONE, ...)` when the condition's `return_fraction < 0` and the table's `state_cone` is `NONNEGATIVE_FLUX` (so SN refuses diffusion's `ZeroFluxCondition` on the state-cone axis without importing it: SN may not import diffusion, an L3 to L3 edge), and `Refused(PLUMBING, ...)` otherwise, naming the table's admitted keys; PLUMBING requires an issue, so a method that lacks a registry entry for a shared unit lists it in its own table with the tracking issue rather than relying on this door.
- **Deck arm.** The one `DeckRealizer.admit/realize` is an exhaustive `match` over `DeckTransformation` with `assert_never`.
- **`stamp_boundary_role(op)`** (`orpheus/transport/boundary_realization.py:121`) moves here unchanged.
- **Name check (`_amend_names.py`).** `RealizerTable`, `ResponseRealizer`, `DeckRealizer`, `StateCone`, `TableNotSealedError`, `SealedTableError`: 0 definitions (`Realizer` as a word: 9 files of prose, `BoundaryRealizer` is the retiring Protocol).


### 2.5 Numerics: the certification, `orpheus/numerics/quadrature/ordinate_pairing.py` (new, unit L4)

Ruling 7 of `layer_dag.md` (the certification predicate to numerics, keyed on (quadrature, rigid motion) with no law argument) stands, and the five leaf units of the workflow plan (DRAFT 1's) omit it. Decision 5 of section 9 puts it in L4: the scaled ordinate permutation of section 2.6 needs the permutation it carries to be a certified one.

```python
class OrdinatePairingError(ValueError): ...
class QuadratureNotClosedUnderMotionError(OrdinatePairingError): ...     # no bijective weight-preserving match (e.g. an odd-n_phi product rule has no x-mirror closure)
class PairingNotMeasurePreservingError(OrdinatePairingError): ...        # ERR-042
class PairingNotInvolutiveError(OrdinatePairingError): ...               # ERR-044
class PairingDoesNotCrossError(OrdinatePairingError): ...                # ERR-045

def ordinate_pairing(quadrature: Quadrature, motion: RigidMotion) -> Permutation
    # the mirror plane is stated ONCE, by the motion: its normal is the -1 eigenvector of `motion.linear`, and the inflow-to-outflow crossing is checked on that axis. A motion that is not an axis reflection raises OrdinatePairingError. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1's third argument `axis: str`: a disagreeing pair (a reflection about x with axis="y") was spellable and certified the wrong crossing (elegance C5); `[M]` today `_specular.py:132` derives the motion FROM the axis (`_mirror_motion(axis, 3)`), so no second spelling existed and the draft would have minted one
```
- **It folds onto `Quadrature.ordinate_permutation`.** `[M]` `orpheus/numerics/quadrature/directional.py:455` already returns `Permutation | None` (`None` when the matching is not a bijection, ERR-073), and `orpheus/geometry/boundary/_specular.py::_specular_pairing` (`:105`) is a wrapper that unwraps `.indices` and raises a `BoundaryError` on `None`. The new function calls `ordinate_permutation(motion)`, raises `QuadratureNotClosedUnderMotionError` on `None`, then asserts on the returned permutation the three claims that keep their teeth independently (the built-in certificate of the relabelling does not subsume them: ERR-044's involution has no carrier in a bijection, and ERR-042's measure is the trace measure `w |mu_a|`, not the bare `w` the matcher compares; `_specular.py:111-130`): measure preservation (relative tolerance `_MEASURE_RTOL = 1e-12`, the existing constant), involution, and inflow-to-outflow crossing on the named axis. It RETURNS the certified `Permutation` (X3: a returned permutation asserts its own bijectivity), and the SN realizer uses that one array: one computation, not a certification beside a second derivation (today `_specular_pairing` derives and three `assert_specular_pairing_*` re-derive).
- **It is called for the two units whose realization is that permutation:** `MirrorDeck`, and the `SpecularChain` of a wall (its image is the `ScaledOrdinatePermutation`). It is NOT called for an empty network: the zero network realizes the `ZeroMap` before any pairing, so a perfectly absorbing wall needs no certified quadrature and `WallCondition.specular(0.0)` is admitted on a quadrature that is not closed under the mirror exactly as `WallCondition.vacuum()` is (`[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1's "also at `albedo == 0.0`, where it certifies the pairing", which made two spellings of `R = 0` differ in admission, elegance V2; the one-type ruling dissolves the twin). A `TranslationDeck` needs no permutation (the identity: ordinate `n` at one face feeds ordinate `n` at the other) and its crossing equality is the exact set equality the SN deck arm already asserts.
- **Placement.** `numerics -> geometry` is the allowed direction (`layer_dag.md` ruling 1); `RigidMotion` and `Permutation` are `orpheus/geometry/transformation.py:336,205`. `AXIS_INDEX` (`geometry/faces.py`) stays where WP-B placed it. `TANGENTIAL_EPS` moved to the numerics leaf `orpheus/numerics/tangential.py` `[LANDED WF2.2]`, so that `quadrature` does not import `spaces` (an intra-package cycle the cycle gate refuses); the orchestrator accepted this file outside L4's set (section 11).
- **Name check.** `ordinate_pairing`, `OrdinatePairingError` and its four subclasses: 0 definitions, 0 word hits anywhere.
- **The ladder's tier names: `TraceRole` gains `"current"` (decision 49; unit L4, numerics, additive).** `[M]` `orpheus/numerics/spaces/angular_trace_space.py:168`: `TraceRole = Literal["full", "outflow", "inflow"]` names three of the ladder's four tiers; the fourth, `current_space`, lives in a separate dict `_current_spaces` keyed by face alone and is already NAMED `angular_trace[<face>:current]` (`:663-700`, `_face_space_name` at `:751` formats `angular_trace[<face>:<role>]`). DRAFT 2's `Rung(Enum): OUTFLOW; CURRENT; INFLOW` was a second spelling of the same tiers (two members shared, one each side unshared; `recheck_elegance.md` B5, the X4 tell "two spellings of one partition"). The one definition is `TraceRole`, widened to `Literal["full", "outflow", "current", "inflow"]`; `_current_spaces` names its spaces through `_face_space_name(face, "current")` (the string is unchanged, so no space equality moves). The widening is inert: `[M]` `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_d3_names_sites.py` finds 8 references to `TraceRole`, all in `angular_trace_space.py` and the `spaces/__init__.py` re-export, 0 `get_args`, `assert_never` or `match` in the file (control: 39 such calls elsewhere in `orpheus/`, so the instrument reads nonzero), 0 reads of `.role` in `orpheus/numerics`; `AngularFaceTraceSpace.role` stays "identity and legibility only, nothing dispatches on it" and gains one reader that CHECKS, never dispatches: the realization functor compares `chain.factors[0].domain` with `outflow.role` and `chain.factors[-1].codomain` with `inflow.role` (S2b gate 20), so the declared composition of `network.py` and the realized composition (`@` on bound operators) meet at one check instead of being two independent spellings of "the rungs compose".

### 2.6 SN: the typed realized operators `orpheus/sn/boundary/realized_operators.py` (new, slice S2b, with the retirement of the old builders: decision 45) and the SN consumers

Today `_BoundBoundaryOperator` (`orpheus/sn/boundary/bound_compat.py:109`) pairs a realized operator with the LAW it came from, and five SN functions ask the law's factors (`law_permutes_ordinates`, `response_kernel.is_zero`, `.amplitude`, `.geometry_map.domain_face`, a `kind` string). The realizer that built the operator already knows each fact (attack elegance A2, B1). So each fact becomes a PROPERTY OF THE TYPE of the realized operator, derived, never a field beside it (adopted point 5). `[REVISED 2026-10-22, orchestrator, DRAFT 3, decision 45]` DRAFT 2 placed this file in the additive leaf L4 and kept the realizer's builders beside it; between L4 and S2b the package would have held the same three bodies twice, a dual spelling of private helpers the thirteenth exchange's own logic for the conditions package refuses, and the Lambertian divisor change of R-11 would have landed in one copy only (`recheck_elegance.md` B1, `recheck_structure.md` frame 3). The file therefore lands in slice S2b, in the commit that retires the realizer's builders, and L4 keeps only the certification. The file is named `realized_operators.py` (C10): `realized.py` beside `realizer.py` is one letter apart, a typo habitat in imports and greps; `closures.py` is taken in SN's vocabulary by the angular closure.

**Realization is a functor on the network (thirteenth exchange).** Each method realizes each factor chain; the typed operators below are the IMAGES of `0`, `s P` and `d (E o Rt)`, and the image of a sum is the sum of the images:

| declaration | image in SN | type |
|---|---|---|
| the empty network (`0`) | the zero map on the face's trace | `ZeroMap` |
| `s * SpecularChain` | `s P_pi`, `pi` the certified ordinate permutation | `ScaledOrdinatePermutation` |
| `d * DiffuseChain` | `d (emission o contraction)`, the factored Lambertian | `RankOneReemission` |
| a sum of chains | the sum of the images | `RealizedSum` |

```python
class RealizedBoundary(LinearOperator, ABC):               # replaces _BoundBoundaryOperator; the algebra object delegates to its derived operator
    face: str                                               # the face whose inflow it fills
    @property @abstractmethod domain_face -> str            # the face whose outflow it consumes
    @property @abstractmethod return_fraction -> float      # J^-/J^+ of the realized map on this face's trace
    @property @abstractmethod permutes_ordinates -> bool    # the operator is a scaled ordinate permutation that is not the identity
    @property is_vacuum -> bool                             # isinstance(self, ZeroMap): R = 0, derived from the TYPE
    @property @abstractmethod inner -> LinearOperator       # DERIVED from the parameters (below), never a constructor argument

# The constructors take the LADDER, not three siblings of it (decision 46; recheck_elegance B2): `trace` is the AngularTraceSpace of the face's method space, and every space and weight a body needs is READ from it.
#   outflow = trace.outflow_space(<domain face>), inflow = trace.inflow_space(face), current = trace.current_space(face).  The ladder's metric is the retraction's covector:
#   `[M]` `_d3_trace_axes.py` (DRAFT 2 cited `_ladder_metric_probe.py`, which does not exist; the fact re-measured and holds): the realizer's re-derived cos_w == outflow_space(face).inner_product_weights bitwise on 14 of 14 (quadrature, face) pairs; `[M]` `_d3_trace_axes.py` re-measures it on 10 of 10 (5 quadratures, 2 faces; control: a 1-ulp perturbation reads unequal).
class ZeroMap(RealizedBoundary):                            # (face, trace): R = 0.  return_fraction 0.0, permutes_ordinates False, domain_face == face
    # inner = ZeroMorphism(domain=outflow, codomain=inflow)   (what `_narrowed_zero_operator` returns, realizer.py:307-385; the morphism derives both zeros from its declared pair)
class ScaledOrdinatePermutation(RealizedBoundary):          # (alpha, permutation, face, partner, trace): R = alpha P_pi.  alpha in (0, 1] (a zero scale is the ZeroMap, refused here).  outflow = trace.outflow_space(partner)
    # inner = alpha * stamp_boundary_role(PermutationOperator(outflow.to_local(permutation.inverse().indices[inflow.ordinate_indices]), axis=0, domain=outflow, codomain=inflow) & IdentityOperator())
    #   (what `_deck_kernel` + `_attenuated_kernel_operator` build, realizer.py:388-434 and :437-621; `to_local` raises on an ordinate that crossed to a non-Gamma_+ index, the ERR-045 guard)
    #   return_fraction = alpha;  domain_face = partner;  permutes_ordinates = (permutation is not the identity) -> a mirror True, a translation wrap False
class RankOneReemission(RealizedBoundary):                  # (alpha, face, trace): R = alpha (emission o contraction).  alpha in (0, 1].  NO norm, NO contraction weights, NO inflow extent: all three are READ from the half-spaces
    # inner = alpha * stamp_boundary_role(IsotropicEmissionOperator(np.sum(inflow.inner_product_weights), inflow.shape[0], domain=current, codomain=inflow)
    #                                      @ PartialCurrentOperator(outflow.inner_product_weights, domain=outflow, codomain=current))
    #   (what `_checked_angular_average` returns, realizer.py:641-724; the divisor is the INFLOW mass by the plain sum, section 2.6 below and R-11)
    #   return_fraction = alpha;  permutes_ordinates False;  domain_face == face
class RealizedSum(RealizedBoundary):                        # (terms: tuple[RealizedBoundary, ...]): the image of a sum of chains.  PARSED: len(terms) >= 2, no ZeroMap term (a zero term is the empty network's image and is never summed), one `face` for all terms (recheck_elegance B9)
    # inner = the sum of the terms' inners;  return_fraction = math.fsum of the terms';  permutes_ordinates False;  is_vacuum False
```
- **Facts derive from parameters; `inner` derives too (elegance C6, structure 5).** DRAFT 1 stored a pre-built `inner: LinearOperator` beside `alpha`, which (a) made "the facts cannot be set by hand" a witness (a lying `alpha` reddens G14) instead of a structure, and (b) made the gate "equality and hash are content" unpassable: `LinearOperator` is a `Protocol` (`orpheus/numerics/operator.py:624`), `PermutationOperator` (`elementary_operators.py:65`) and `RankOneOperator` (`:819`) have no `ContentIdentity`, `content.py:425` refuses them, and the only way to green was a `compare=False` field, the call-order hazard of L-023. Now each typed operator holds its PARAMETERS `(alpha, ..., face, trace)` and OWNS the one body of its operator, built on the operator algebra that exists (`ZeroMorphism`; `PermutationOperator` gathered through the half-space's `to_local`; `IsotropicEmissionOperator @ PartialCurrentOperator`), from spaces it reads off the ladder; the realizer's builders are not called and not kept, they RETIRE in the same slice (the next bullet). `return_fraction == alpha` is then a theorem of the construction and G14 stays as the array witness of the builder; a lying `alpha` is unspellable. The realized types are NOT `ContentIdentity`: they compare by their parameters (`type`, `face`, `partner`, `alpha`, the permutation's index bytes) and hash the same; the roster gate does not cover them, and S2b gate 17 states that.

### 2.7 Diffusion: `orpheus/diffusion/zero_flux.py` (new, slice S3) and `DIFFUSION_REALIZERS`

`ZeroFluxCondition(BoundaryCondition)`, in `orpheus/diffusion/zero_flux.py` (C10: a module `diffusion/boundary_conditions.py` beside the package `transport/boundary_conditions/` is the homonym `layer_dag.md` ruling 7 refused for the package name; the type is `ZeroFluxCondition`, not `ZeroFluxBoundary`, so that no new class shares a qualname with the old `geometry/boundary/zero_flux.py` class that lives until WF2.4: `[M]` the old class is named by 8 production files and 10 test files, among them `tests/gates/derivations/test_characteristic_walls.py` of slice S5, so it cannot be deleted in S3 and the rename is the move that keeps the qualname gate honest, decision 30). No fields, `return_fraction = -1.0` (the partial-current statement `J^- = -J^+`), `face_local = True`, `source` empty; diffusion's table registers it, nothing else does (ruling 5). It is outside the sub-Markov cone by definition, which is why the cone is `WallCondition`'s parse and not a base invariant. A method-only response stays its own type (thirteenth exchange): it is not a chain.

`DIFFUSION_REALIZERS = RealizerTable("diffusion", state_cone=StateCone.SIGNED_CURRENT)`, in `orpheus/diffusion/boundary_realizer.py` (the `DiffusionBoundaryRealizer` class, `:188`, and its `_partial_current_albedo`, `:226`, retire; `_albedo_operator`, `:170`, stays). Diffusion annihilates the shape (it sees `Pi_P1`: `(s, d) -> s + d`), so both chains realize as the scalar weight times the identity and a sum of chains is the scalar sum:

| realizer | `realizes` | admission |
|---|---|---|
| `DiffusionZero` | `ZeroNetwork` | `Realized(faithful=True)`, tier 1 |
| `DiffusionSpecularChain` | `SpecularChain` | `Realized(faithful=False)`, tier 2 (the shape is annihilated by `Pi`); the realized scalar is the weight |
| `DiffusionDiffuseChain` | `DiffuseChain` | `Realized(faithful=False)`, tier 2; the realized scalar is the weight |
| `DiffusionZeroFlux` | `ZeroFluxCondition` | `Realized(faithful=True)`, tier 1 (`A = -1`) |
| `DiffusionSource` (a `SourceRealizer`) | `InflowTerms` | `Refused(PLUMBING, issue=656)`, no body to stub (`sample` has a default and the door raises before it): the fixed-source arm; lifts with no theory change. (`[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` DRAFT 1 cited #290, which is CLOSED: `gh issue view 290`; #656 tracks diffusion's affine channel.) |
| `DiffusionDecks` | `DeckTransformation` | `MirrorDeck`: `Realized(faithful=False)`, tier 2 (`A = 1`); `TranslationDeck`: `Refused(TOPOLOGY)` (a per-face scalar has no slot for cross-face coupling) |

The `DiffusionCombined` row of DRAFT 1 is gone: a mixed wall is the functor's sum of its chains' scalars, `A = s + d`, `Realized(False)`.
- **Diffusion times vacuum is `Realized(faithful=True)`, not `Approximated("Marshak")` (measured).** `[M]` `_probe_diffusion_tier.py` called `DiffusionBoundaryRealizer().realize` on 9 law spellings (all 8 law classes, plus two albedo re-emission spellings): 7 realized, 2 raised `BoundaryError`. The deciding line is `return response.amplitude` in `_partial_current_albedo` (`diffusion/boundary_realizer.py:332`): `VacuumInflow` has a scalar response of amplitude 0.0 and a self-paired deck, and it realizes as `ZeroOperator`; `J^- = 0` maps to `A = 0` exactly, and a scalar commutes with every linear projection. "Marshak" is the P1 closure (`J^+- = phi/4 +- J/2` plus Fick's law), applied identically to albedo and reflective, so it belongs to the method and not to the vacuum law's admission. The control `WallCondition.specular(0.4)` is unambiguously tier 1 by the rule's own text (alpha recovered as `0.4 I`) and is classified tier 2 here because diffusion cannot tell it from `.white(0.4)`; the two spellings with one image are the tier-2 witness. White, reflective and albedo images coincide in diffusion because diffusion sees only `response.amplitude` (tier 2), expected. The refusals: prescribed inflow (affine, plumbing), periodic (the translation deck, `PairedDeck`, topological).
- **Bare albedo.** Diffusion alone could read a shape-free alpha; the new type has no shape field, so the diffusion-only tests that wrote `AlbedoBoundary(a)` or `BC("albedo", {"albedo": a})` (`[M]` 77 shapeless constructions: 76 in tests, 1 production, `transport/method.py:381`, per `attack_math.md`'s AST pass) take `WallCondition.white(a)` (the physical default of a diffuse exterior), stated once in the codemod (section 6.0). 2-D diffusion stays refused wholesale (`orpheus/diffusion/augmented_mesh.py:210-211`, 1-D only).

- **Name check.** `WallAmplitudes`: 0 definitions (`_amend_names.py`), `.claude/` prose only; `from_amplitudes`: 0 definitions. `Wall` and `Walls` stay (`walls.py:77,121`).

### 2.10 The method heads and the shared body (slices S2 to S4)

`TransportMethod` (`orpheus/transport/method.py:~150`) replaces `BOUNDARY_OPERATOR_REGISTRY: ClassVar[dict[str, type[BoundaryTraceLaw]]]` with `boundary_realizers: ClassVar[RealizerTable]` and a `binding: Binding` attribute. `resolve_boundary_conditions(method)` becomes `realize_boundary(method) -> dict[str, OpT]`:
```python
def realize_boundary(method):                        # the ONE generic body
    table, binding, realized = method.boundary_realizers, method.binding, {}
    for face in binding.faces:                        # EVERY face, including a face with no trace slot (the hollow radial inner face)
        declared = binding.decks[face] if face in binding.decks else binding.condition_of(face)
        if face in face_labels(method.axes):          # the trace's inventory (mesh/axis.py), one name (DRAFT 1 also wrote trace_faces)
            realized[face] = method.realize_boundary_role(declared, face)   # builds the method space; table.realize calls admit(declared, site, space) first
        else:
            table.require(declared, method.face_site(face))                  # type- and site-level admission only (space=None); raises BoundaryRefused
    return realized
```
`RealizerTable.require(declared, site, space=None)` is `admit` plus the raise (one definition of the refusal message). The hollow inner face has no trace and so no `realize`: for it `require` is the only door (law L-D carries the carve-out).
- **Binding at construction.** The head calls `bind(carrier, conditions)` with its carrier: SN and diffusion pass their `MaterialMesh` (whose `face_roles` property is `roles_of_axes(self.axes)`, including a hollow radial axis's inner role, and whose `symmetry` is below), CP, MoC and MC pass their `Mesh1D`. `MaterialMesh._axis_key`/`_law_key` (`orpheus/transport/mesh/material_mesh.py:81,96`) key the axes by their ROLES (a deck's content or an `int`); the method head's `_contractibility_key` adds `binding.declared`'s conditions in tag order (the face-to-condition join is derived, so the key reads the one declared map and does not re-walk two maps: C3), each folded through the SAME try/except `_law_key` has today (`hash(condition)`; on `TypeError`, the key `(type qualname, id)`), so two problems with the same mesh and different conditions are different phase spaces, as they are today (the laws are in `_axis_key`), and a condition holding an open sampler (the three measured implementors) keys by object instead of raising. `Binding` itself is a `ContentIdentity` value whose own digest raises `ContentlessError` for such a condition; the head never calls it (decision 16). `MaterialMesh.symmetry` is a property: one `AxisMesh` (Cartesian) passes the cell edges and `mat_map` values (raw; `colouring_symmetry` merges the runs), a `RadialAxisMesh` the radial identity, anything else `UncomputedSymmetries`.
- **Method hooks.** `realize_boundary_law(law, face)` becomes `realize_boundary_role(role, face)`: the method builds its space for `face` and calls `table.realize`. The Protocol's other members are unchanged.
- **Entry signatures.** `boundary_conditions` is keyword-only, trailing, default empty, on: `SNProblem.__init__`, `SNProblem.from_axes`, `SNProblem.from_material_mesh`, `solve_sn`, `solve_sn_adjoint`, `solve_sn_adjoint_fixed_source`, `solve_sn_fixed_source`, `solve_sn_multiplying_source`, `DiffusionMesh.__init__`, `solve_diffusion_1d`, `CPMesh.__init__`, `solve_cp`, `MCMesh.__init__`, `solve_monte_carlo` (through its `MCMesh`), `MOCMesh.__init__`, `solve_moc`, and `GeometrySpecification` (as the separate `exterior`). `[M]` 825 method entry calls in tests (design B `probe_consumers.out`: `SNProblem` 461, `solve_sn` 144, `solve_cp` 45, `MOCMesh` 30, `GeometrySpecification` 29, `DiffusionMesh` 28, `solve_monte_carlo` 24, `CPMesh` 18, `solve_diffusion_1d` 14, `solve_sn_adjoint` 14, `MCMesh` 11, `solve_moc` 7), of which only the sites with a tag gain an argument; the deck-only sites gain nothing.
- **`AngularBoundarySourceSink.from_problem_laws(problem)`** (`orpheus/transport/fields/angular_boundary_source_sink.py:415`, a duck read of an SN object from transport) becomes `from_binding(binding, trace, realizers)`, a transport object read in transport: the method passes its own realizer table (`TransportMethod.boundary_realizers`, the method's `RealizerTable`), and `from_binding` samples every tag's inflow through `realizers.sample_inflow(term, face, trace)`, so transport names no method table (orchestrator, DRAFT 3.1; `verify_draft3.md` repair 3).

Each law names where it is gated and the input that reddens it first (X1). "HEAD" is `5d04c296`.
