## 4. The admission table, as data

### 4.1 What was measured (the grid the table is derived from)

Source: `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/measure_admission_table.md`, `[M]` 322 constructions over 14 (method, geometry) pairs (SN: slab, cylinder, sphere, hollow cylinder; diffusion: slab, cylinder, sphere; CP: slab, cylinder, sphere; MoC: cylinder, slab; MC: slab, cylinder) times 18 typed declarations plus 5 tags (`_probe_admission.py`), every realized typed cell solved on a homogeneous one-group medium with `k_inf = 0.6` (`_probe_admission.py --numeric`), plus 20 2-D probes, 36 fixed-source probes and 6 combination probes. Positive controls fired: the refusal path (SN refused `ZeroFluxBoundary` on 4 of 4 geometries; CP refused the mirror on 3 of 3), the realize path (SN mirror `k = 0.6 = k_inf`), and an ordered physical control (SN slab `k`: vacuum 0.491 < albedo 0.5 0.536 < mirror 0.600), so a flat or inverted `k` column would have shown a broken probe. Homogeneous has no boundary: `git grep -il "boundary|BC|albedo|face_laws" -- orpheus/homogeneous` returns 0 files, so its 10 cells are N/A.

The verdict rule: a cell is about the realization of the declared exterior, not about the method's interior error against transport. Over 6 methods times 10 declarations (60 cells) the verdicts are faithful F 18, named approximation A 2 (MoC mirror, MC translation on a cylinder, both #547 DOMAIN; amended: MC's is now `Refused(TOPOLOGY, #547)`, section 4.3 delta 9), refused R 40. Defects are flagged beside a verdict and are not a fourth verdict. The 40 refusals by axis: plumbing 36, topology 2 (SN curvilinear translation, diffusion translation), state cone 2 (SN zero flux, Cartesian and curvilinear), angular resolution 0.

### 4.2 The tables, post-carve (data for S2a's pin and for S2 to S4)

Site classes (the `FaceSite` key): `CART` (any Cartesian face), `RAD_OUTER` (a solid or hollow radial body's outer face), `RAD_INNER` (a hollow radial body's inner face, a tag after D5). A `FaceSite.site_class` property (an Enum of these three geometric classes, derived once from `(face, coord, dimension, hollow_inner)`) is the discriminator the realizers and this pin read (elegance N14: otherwise each `admit` re-derives "the hollow inner" from the fields, a repeated conditional). DRAFT 2 listed a fourth member, `CART_LEFT` (a Cartesian slab's `xmin`): that is CP's own rule (its slab kernel's forced mirror) lifted into the Enum every method shares, an abstraction lifted over the difference between its instances (`recheck_elegance.md` B9); CP's realizer reads `site.face == "xmin"` on a `CART` site, and the pin keys the CP column on `(site_class, face)` (decision 56). A cell is `Realized(T)` (faithful), `Realized(F)` (tier 2), `Approximated(names, issues)`, `Refused(axis, issue)`; "n/a" means the combination is not spellable (a deck on a radial face is a geometry `DeckError`, section 2.2). `#653`, `#654`, `#655`, `#656` are the issues filed before this amendment (`gh issue view`, 2026-10-22, all OPEN): #653 the SN radial corner action for the diffuse return and an inflow (DRAFT 1's F1); #654 the CP, MoC and MC boundary tables hold only what their old string registries held (F2); #655 MC's pitch is decoupled from the declared extent (F3); #656 diffusion's affine boundary source has no channel (replaces the closed #290).

**The rows are VALUE CLASSES of the one shared type, plus the method-only types** (DRAFT 1 keyed its rows by the declaration type, `AlbedoBoundary(a, Specular)` and so on, which no longer exist). A value class is read off the condition: its network (empty, specular only, diffuse only, both) and its source (zero or not); a wall's admission is the meet over its nonzero units (section 2.4, "realization"). So the "mixed" row is not an independent entry: it is the meet of the specular and diffuse rows, and the pin states it so that a change to either row edits it.

| value class | SN `CART` | SN `RAD_OUTER` | SN `RAD_INNER` | diffusion (1-D, any site) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `MirrorDeck` | `Realized(T)` (certified by `ordinate_pairing`, else `Refused(ANGULAR_RESOLUTION)`) | n/a | n/a | `Realized(F)` (tier 2, `A = 1`) |
| `TranslationDeck` | `Realized(T)` | n/a | n/a | `Refused(TOPOLOGY)` |
| empty network, no source (vacuum, `specular(0)`, `white(0)`) | `Realized(T)` | `Realized(T)` | `Refused(PLUMBING, #511)` | `Realized(T)` (tier 1) |
| specular only, weight `w` | `Realized(T)` | `Realized(T)` | `Realized(T)` iff `w == 1.0` (the cavity the closure computes), else `Refused(PLUMBING, #511)` | `Realized(F)` (tier 2) |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| `ZeroFluxCondition` | `Refused(STATE_CONE)` | `Refused(STATE_CONE)` | `Refused(STATE_CONE)` | `Refused(STATE_CONE)` | `Refused(STATE_CONE)` |

A MoC slab, a hollow body and any non-cylinder are refused by MoC's own guard (#514), not by admission. MC's hollow cylinder is refused by its guard (#513). Homogeneous: N/A.

### 4.3 The deltas from the measured grid and from design B

1. **Diffusion times vacuum is `Realized(True)`**, not `Approximated` (section 2.7).
2. **CP times vacuum is `Realized(True)`** (D4). The defect stays visible as three existing strict `xfail` rows plus the new vacuum-versus-white row (section 6, S4).
3. **CP times the diffuse chain is `Realized(True)` at weight `1.0` only**; below that it is `Refused(PLUMBING, #654)`. This turns a silent wrong answer into a refusal, a behaviour change announced at the S4 checkpoint. The spelling of the same physics as a shaped albedo (refused today as `kind == "albedo"`) becomes admitted at weight `1.0` by the D1 fold.
4. **SN curvilinear times the diffuse chain and times a source are refused at `admit`** (#653), not at the first sweep. `[M]` `SNProblem` constructs these and `solve_sn`/`solve_sn_fixed_source` refuse (12 of 12; the vacuum, specular and mirror rows solved 12 of 12): the radial characteristic's corner action is ruled only for a permuting law or a zero return (`_has_ruled_corner_action`, `orpheus/sn/operators/boundary.py:116`, `NotImplementedError` at `:985`).
5. **The key includes the face** (the measurement's finding b): CP's slab left face is a forced mirror, SN's hollow inner face is mirror-only today, MC's period must equal the slab width (the third is a guard, not an admission: decision 13).
6. **Mixed walls are bindable and admitted in SN Cartesian and diffusion** (D2; one type, so there is no combination row, only the meet of the units). `[M]` through the walker door both methods realized all three probes (`0.5/0.5`, the super-Markov `0.6 + 0.6`, the signed `-0.5 + 1.0`), 6 of 6, unchecked; D2 closes the latter two at construction. Slab only; the radial row is `[R]`.
7. **Decks on radial faces are not spellable**; the cells that were "SN curvilinear mirror `Realized`" (cylinder mirror fixed-source `k = 150.80`) become the specular wall tag with the same matrix (the reflective cleanup's carry check held 106 of 106; the fixture is re-captured, section 6.0).
8. **`ZeroFluxCondition` in CP, MoC and MC is `Refused(STATE_CONE)`** (the table's unregistered-key door), where the measurement counted it plumbing: three cells flip axis, which is the correct reading (their state is a non-negative cone).
9. **MoC's specular wall stays `Approximated` and MC's becomes a refusal.** MoC computes a specular return, on a different shape (an equal-area square cell with reflecting flat faces): a named DOMAIN approximation (#547); the ruling of the thirteenth exchange is silent on MoC and the row is unchanged in substance. MC computes a TRANSLATION of a square cell (a torus wrap), which no 1-D declaration describes: its specular-wall cell on a cylinder is `Refused(TOPOLOGY, ..., #547)` and the three tests that declare it are strict `xfail`s (decision 31, replacing DRAFT 1's provisional `Approximated` row). The axis is `TOPOLOGY` and not `PLUMBING` because there IS theory behind the refusal: the carrier MC samples is a torus, whose deck group is an image lattice, and the declared body (a cylinder) has none (`[R]` the cross-method pollination note of the structure check: "a deck realized by an image sum over a group the carrier does not declare"); `[RATIFIED 2026-10-22, orchestrator, DRAFT 3, decision 55]`: the axis is `TOPOLOGY`. The refusal axis is a question about the METHOD'S CARRIER, and the topological axis's question is "can its operator couple two faces"; MC realizes a different DOMAIN (a torus wrap of a square cell, whose opposite faces ARE coupled by an image lattice) from the one declared (a cylinder, whose faces are not), so the declaration is refused on the axis whose question MC's carrier answers differently from the declared body. It is not `ANGULAR_RESOLUTION` (MC resolves angle by sampling) and not `STATE_CONE` (a specular return is non-negative), and not `PLUMBING` (theory stands behind the refusal).

### 4.4 The pin

`tests/gates/transport/test_admission_matrix.py` (S2a creates the file with the mechanism's test-local rows; S2b, S3 and S4 each append their method's rows) pins the table EXACTLY, one row per (method, value class, site class, weight class), so every change to an admission edits the pin; the "both chains" row is asserted equal to the meet of the two single-chain rows. A `Refused(PLUMBING)` without an issue number raises at construction (`Refused.__post_init__`: PLUMBING requires `issue`), so "not built yet" can never be an unexplained refusal. `[M]` the cited issue numbers #511, #513, #514, #546, #547, #653, #654, #655, #656 were each read with `gh issue view --json state` on 2026-10-22: 9 of 9 OPEN. `Refused.__post_init__` cannot check liveness; a pin test can (one `gh` read per cited number is the archivist's at WF2.5, or the admission pin lists the numbers for the orchestrator).

## 6. The carve (WF2.3): slices S1 to S5, sequential on one branch

Mechanics (`workflow_orchestration.md`, WF2.3): S1 runs `build-units` with no `worktree` (it makes the branch's worktree on `refactor/wave2-boundary-carve`, which already holds L1, L4 and L5); each later slice passes that path as its unit's `worktree`. There is NO dual spelling: the geometry and mesh API changes outright in S1 and a consumer either reads the new API or is red until its slice. The tree is red between slices for every package whose slice has not run, green for the slice's own package; it is green again, and merged `--ff-only`, only after S5, WF2.4 and the full bar. Each slice is reviewed (qa and elegance, Fable) before the next starts, so a defect is caught in the slice that made it. The orchestrator reads each result before launching the next; no ruling inside a workflow (invariant 6): a question goes to `NEEDS`.

### 6.0 What every slice shares

**The codemod** (applied by script per call site, the helper-mediated sites by hand; `[M]` sizing from `probe_consumers.out`: 1139 `BC(...)` and 833 `BC.<kind>` reads, 626 typed-law constructions, 206 oriented constructions, 281 mesh/axis constructions passing a law keyword, 825 method entry calls; about 130 `BC` values pass through test-local helpers and 108 sit at module scope).
- **R1 tags.** Faces whose old declaration was one value at one site share one tag; tags are numbered `0, 1, ...` in face-inventory order; the solve at that site receives `boundary_conditions={tag: condition}`. Exception: every `PrescribedInflow` face gets its OWN tag, so the codemod never manufactures a symmetry claim the old code did not make (law L-C). `[M]` 3 dicts hold two or more `PrescribedInflow` (`tests/gates/sn/operators/test_operator_block_role.py:158` and two others) and none shares a tag after R1.
- **R2.** `BC.reflective`, `BC("reflective")` or `ReflectiveBoundary(...)` on a Cartesian face is `MirrorDeck()`. On a curved face it is a tag bound to `WallCondition.specular(1.0)` (the meaning change, Appendix B). A reflective declaration with an albedo (`ReflectiveBoundary(axis, albedo != 1)` retired in the reflective cleanup, `99b9847d`) is already `AlbedoBoundary(a, SpecularReturn(axis))`, which is `WallCondition.specular(a)`.
- **R3.** `BC("periodic")` or `PeriodicBoundary(...)` on a Cartesian face is `TranslationDeck()`. On a curved face it is unspellable (Appendix B): a refusal test of `DeckError`, or (MC) R2's tag with the strict `xfail` of decision 31.
- **R4.** `BC.vacuum`, `VacuumInflow()` are `WallCondition.vacuum()`; `BC.white`, `WhiteBoundary(albedo=a, ...)` are `WallCondition.white(a)`; `AlbedoBoundary(a, SpecularReturn())` is `WallCondition.specular(a)` and `AlbedoBoundary(a, IsotropicReturn())` is `WallCondition.white(a)`; `BC("albedo", {"albedo": a})` is `WallCondition.white(a)` (the physical default of a diffuse exterior; the shape is invisible to diffusion, the only method that admitted the bare tag); `BC("partial", {"albedo": a})` is `WallCondition.specular(a)`; `BC("zero_flux")` and `ZeroFluxBoundary()` are `ZeroFluxCondition()`; `PrescribedInflow(source=s)` is `WallCondition.inflow(v)` where `ConstantInflowSource(v)` is the uniform number `v`, and an `evaluate(space)` object is wrapped in the frame its author meant (`WallCondition.framed(FaceFrameInflow(s))` unless it reads laboratory directions: `_ManufacturedFaceInflow` reads `space.directions` and is `LabFrameInflow`); a sum or scaling of laws (`LawSum`, `LawScaled`) is `+` and `*` on `WallCondition` (negative coefficients are `WallDisplacement`); the 4 `BC("marshak")` refusal probes become probes with a test-local `BoundaryCondition` subclass registered nowhere.
- **R5.** Orientation arguments are dropped (206 sites). Tests whose subject WAS an orientation mismatch (B3.4a, B3.4c, `crossed_diagnosis`; `[M]` 10 files, design B section 3.4) lose that subject; each deletion carries its D.14 demotion check, and law L-E (naturality of the mirror) is the replacement witness.
- **R6 inner faces (D5).** An `inner=` declaration of any value becomes an integer tag; the test binds the condition it means; the 1-D radial reflective cavity SN computes is `WallCondition.specular(1.0)`. `[M]` `_curved_sites.py`: 39 `inner=` declarations on a curved body, of which 23 are reflective and 2 periodic (these 25 are D5's "25 hollow inner faces"); the other 14 hold other values (vacuum and similar) and were accepted by the geometry-only consumers (the references) and refused by the SN adapter (`NotImplementedError`, 15 of 15 constructible non-reflective typed laws, `measure_admission_table.md`); all 39 become tags.
- **R7 errors.** `err.law == "..."` becomes `isinstance(err.declaration, ...)` or `err.declaration == MirrorDeck()`; `raises(BoundaryError)` on a certification failure re-points to the numerics error.
- **R8 keywords.** `face_laws=` is `face_roles=`; `bc_low/bc_high/bc_outer` are `role_low/role_high/role_outer`; `outer_law` is `outer_role`; `boundaries=` is `face_roles=`.

**The carry fixture.** Captured at `main` `5d04c296` in a detached worktree BEFORE S1 (script `tests/gates/transport/_capture_carry_head.py`, output `tests/gates/transport/carry_head.npz`, both committed with S2's gate commit, the capture itself done from the HEAD tree): for SN on a slab, a cylinder and a sphere, the eigenvalue `k` and the scalar flux under vacuum, mirror/specular `alpha in {0.3, 0.7, 1}`, isotropic `alpha in {0.5, 1}` (Cartesian only), and the fixed-source solve under a uniform inflow; the realized operator matrix of every face on an S16 slab for each of those; diffusion `k` on the three charts under vacuum, mirror, albedo 0.5 and zero flux; CP `k` under vacuum and white on the three charts; MoC `k` on the cylinder mirror; MC `k` on the slab periodic with the fixed seed. Plus the seven `tests/gates/geometry/snapshots/bc_equivalence_*.npz` snapshots already in the tree (`specular_x_lebedev17`, `specular_y_partial_07_LS6`, `periodic_lebedev17`, `vacuum_lebedev17`, `white_xmax_LS4`, `white_xmin_partial_03_GL`, `mixed_30spec_70white_LS4`): these are a free HEAD fixture for the SN realized operators, and the re-posed `test_bc_equivalence_snapshot.py` (S2) builds the same declarations on the new types and requires bit equality to the existing `.npz` files (the `mixed_30spec_70white` snapshot is the `RealizedSum` witness). Bit-identical (`array_equal`) is the standard: a non-bit difference is a finding to investigate, not a tolerance to widen. The declared exceptions are exactly those of section 8.

**What each slice's report states:** the gates with their reds, the targeted command and its tail, the sites of its files that were re-read by hand, and the meaning-change sites it owned with their new spelling.

### 6.2 S2: transport and SN (two commits: S2a the conditions package, S2b the SN consumers)

**S2a, the conditions package as a move (gates first).** `orpheus/transport/boundary_conditions/` of section 2.4 (all its modules, `from_amplitudes` included) arrives as the first commits of S2, before any SN consumer is touched; the package is green in isolation (it imports L1 for real, `numerics` and `values`), its tests live in `tests/gates/transport/`, and it needs no stand-in for the deck types (DRAFT 1's L2/L3 gates that annotated `FaceIsometry` under `TYPE_CHECKING` and used test-local stand-ins are re-posed on the real L1 types). Sizing, not a reason: S2 is the largest slice (227 test files) and S2a is a self-contained prefix of it. - **The #546 strict xfail, spelled out (D4).** New file `tests/gates/cp/test_cp_vacuum_not_above_white.py`:
```python
_REASON = (
    "#546: CPSolver.compute_keff divides production by net removal with no leakage term, so a vacuum-bounded "
    "eigenvalue lies above the white-bounded one on the same body (measured 2026-10-22 on the 2G A|B body, cp_body, "
    "uniform_volume(4)+(4), CPParams(keff_tol=1e-11): slab 1.4003162 vs 1.2308420 (gap 0.1695), cylinder 0.9354010 vs "
    "0.7393875 (gap 0.1960), sphere 0.5854991 vs 0.4140867 (gap 0.1714)); physics requires k_vacuum <= k_white. "
    "Not fixed by the user's ruling of 2026-10-10 (CP's pencil is a later campaign); D4 of the twelfth exchange: no masking."
)
_VACUUM_ABOVE_WHITE = [pytest.param(c, id=_CHART[c], marks=pytest.mark.xfail(strict=True, reason=_REASON)) for c in (CARTESIAN, CYLINDRICAL, SPHERICAL)]

@pytest.mark.l1
@pytest.mark.parametrize("coord", _VACUUM_ABOVE_WHITE)
def test_a_vacuum_bounded_cp_eigenvalue_does_not_exceed_the_white_bounded_one(coord):
    k_vac = _k(coord, WallCondition.vacuum()); k_white = _k(coord, WallCondition.white())
    assert k_vac <= k_white, f"{_CHART[coord]}: k_vacuum {k_vac!r} exceeds k_white {k_white!r} by {k_vac - k_white:.6e}"

@pytest.mark.l1
def test_the_control_sn_satisfies_the_same_inequality():     # the assertion CAN pass: the same comparison through SN holds on the same body
    ...                                                       # solve_sn with WallCondition.vacuum() vs WallCondition.white(): k_vac < k_white on all three charts

@pytest.mark.foundation
def test_the_vacuum_above_white_rows_are_strict_xfails():
    assert len(_VACUUM_ABOVE_WHITE) == 3
    for param in _VACUUM_ABOVE_WHITE:
        xfails = [m for m in param.marks if m.name == "xfail"]
        assert len(xfails) == 1 and xfails[0].kwargs.get("strict") is True, param.id
        assert "#546" in xfails[0].kwargs.get("reason", ""), param.id
```
  `_k` builds `cp_body(coord, (0.0, 0.5, 1.0), (2, 0), outer)` meshed by `uniform_volume(4)` per region, with the materials `{2: get_mixture("A", "2g"), 0: get_mixture("B", "2g")}` and `boundary_conditions={0: outer}` (on the slab the left face is `MirrorDeck()` and the right tag `0`), as `tests/gates/cp/test_cp_first_collision.py:_vacuum_mesh` does. `[M]` `scratch/layer_dag/wave2_spec/_probe_546_xfail.py` (prints `orpheus.__file__` first) at `5d04c296`: `vac <= white` is False on 3 of 3 charts, so the three rows XFAIL today; when #546 is fixed they XPASS, strictness turns them red, and the marks are deleted. This test is NOT redundant with the existing `test_the_vacuum_k_is_the_eigenvalue_of_the_cp_pencil` (`test_cp_first_collision.py:157`, vacuum `k` against CP's own dense pencil): that compares CP with its own pencil, this compares vacuum with white, a statement a reader can check without CP's internals. Both stay.
- **The #547 strict xfails (the user's ruling of the thirteenth exchange; replaces DRAFT 1's provisional decision 11).** MC on a cylinder computes a periodic square lattice cell (a torus wrap) that no 1-D declaration describes. The 3 MC cylinder sites (`tests/gates/mc/test_gaps.py:692`, `tests/gates/mc/test_properties.py:157`, `:205`; Appendix B rows `:691`, `:156`, `:204` are the same declarations by the AST pass) declare the cylinder's outer face as a tag bound to `WallCondition.specular(1.0)`, the same declaration MoC uses on that cell. MC's table refuses it, `Refused(TOPOLOGY, "MC realizes a periodic equal-area square lattice cell (a torus wrap), not the declared cylinder; no 1-D declaration describes it", issue=547)`, and the three tests are marked `@pytest.mark.xfail(strict=True, raises=BoundaryRefused, reason="#547: ...")`, the reason naming what MC computes and the geometry value that would describe it. They turn red (XPASS, strict) the day MC takes the 2-D geometry value that describes what it computes, and `raises=BoundaryRefused` makes any OTHER failure (a different exception, a wrong number) a hard failure and not an expected one. A companion `foundation` test, in the style of `test_the_vacuum_above_white_rows_are_strict_xfails`, asserts the three marks carry `strict=True`, `raises=BoundaryRefused` and `"#547"` in the reason. The 3 refusal tests (`test_characteristic_walls.py:212-216`, S5) are not MC and follow the `DeckError` path. `[M]` positive control: the three sites exist at `5d04c296` (Appendix B rows).

### 6.5 S5: derivations, specification and reference

- **Production files (A.1, S5):** `orpheus/derivations/{common/reference_body.py, common/continuous_reference.py, continuous/characteristic/walls.py, continuous/fn_method/moment_space.py, continuous/galerkin_spectral/basis_space.py, continuous/mms/moc.py, continuous/mms/sn.py, continuous/peierls_nystrom/slab.py, continuous/singular_eigenfunction/spectrum.py, continuous/sood_registry/case.py}`, `orpheus/specification/specification.py`.
- **Re-spells:** `GeometrySpecification.exterior` (2.9); `Walls.of(geometry, exterior)`; `Wall` composing `WallAmplitudes` (decision 54); `specular_albedos(geometry, exterior, *, owner)` and `require_vacuum`; the three reference consumers gain `exterior`; `mms/sn.py` (14), `mms/moc.py`, `sood_registry/case.py` (3) state tags and `WallAmplitudes`; `continuous_reference.BoundaryCondition` (the `Literal` alias) is renamed `ReferenceBoundaryKind`, `ProblemSpec.boundary_conditions: dict[str, ...]` (`continuous_reference.py:164`, which keeps the production keyword's exact name across a string-valued plain-data edge) is renamed `boundary_kinds` with it (C10), and the alias's value set gains `"zero_flux"` (the latent type lie of `cases/diffusion.py:749,924`, which the alias never listed: `[M]` design A section 11, finding 5). `tests/gates/_reference_pairing.py::conditions_of` is created here. `TAG_REGISTRY`, `_law_of`, `_wall_of`, `_parameters`, `specular_albedo`, `BC.to_alpha` have no remaining reader.
- **Tests (A.2, S5: 36 files, `tests/gates/derivations` 20, `specification` 3, ...; plus `tests/gates/numerics/test_traced_memo_source_index.py`, which names `Wall(` and no retiring symbol: decision 54).** The pairing tests use `tests/gates/_reference_pairing.py::conditions_of`.
- **Meaning-change checkpoint (the user, after S5).** The orchestrator lists for the user, from Appendix B, every site whose meaning changed with its new spelling: the 77 outer plus 23 inner reflective-on-a-curved-face sites (100 literal, by `_curved_sites.py`; design B counts 101: the re-count owed at S1's tip, decision 22) now specular walls (tags bound to `WallCondition.specular(1.0)`), and the 6 outer plus 2 inner periodic-on-a-curved-face sites (8 in both counts) now unspellable (a `DeckError` refusal test, or MC's R2 tag). The 14 non-reflective inner sites become tags bound to what each test means.
- **Done-when gates:** (1) `test_the_specification_is_plain_data_and_complete` (law L-J): a tag missing from `exterior` raises, an extra entry raises, `WallAmplitudes(0.5, 0.5)` raises, two specifications differing only in `exterior` have different digests (red at base: `GeometrySpecification` has no `exterior`; the control is a planted extra field); (2) `test_every_registered_specification_round_trips`: every specification the reference registry builds at HEAD, re-posed with face roles and an `exterior`, yields a bit-identical reference solution (the carry for the references; population = the registry's specifications, listed by the test); (3) `test_walls_of_reads_decks_and_amplitudes`: mirror, wrap, vacuum, partial specular, Lambertian walls identical to the HEAD `Walls.of(geometry)` of the equivalent laws; (4) `test_a_curved_periodic_declaration_is_a_deck_error` (the 3 refusal tests of `test_characteristic_walls.py:212-216` re-posed: the refusal moves from `Walls.__post_init__` to geometry construction); (5) `test_the_wall_message_has_one_owner` (law L-G); (6) `test_references_import_no_production`: `derivations` imports nothing from `transport` or `orpheus.geometry.boundary`; the insulation gate `test_closed_references_import_only_the_numerics_interface` stays green; (7) the specification content pin (section 8, R-2); (8) `test_the_reference_pairing_loop`: `conditions_of({1: WallAmplitudes(0.3, 0), 2: WallAmplitudes(0, 0.7)})` is a `BoundaryConditions` of `WallCondition.specular(0.3)` and `WallCondition.white(0.7)` with the key set preserved, and `ProblemSpec` has no field named `boundary_conditions` (C10).

## 9. Decisions for the orchestrator

Each choice the inputs left open, with the structural reason. Items 1 to 5 and 11 to 13 depart from a design draft or settle a conflict between the attacks; the rest fill a gap. Nothing here overrides a user ruling; where the letter of a ruling and the tree disagree it is stated.

1. **Admission members and axes (conflict: attack elegance S1 against adopted point 8).** `Realized(faithful)`, `Approximated(name, issue)`, `Refused(axis, reason, issue)` with the axes angular resolution, topology, state cone, plumbing; a separate `NotBuilt` member is not minted and `PLUMBING` requires an issue number. Reason: the ruling says "not built yet is not an axis"; the theory page says "exactly one of three axes" and calls `q` "a fourth thing, not on any of these axes"; a refusal with an issue number is the audit trail a `NotBuilt` member would have held. Diffusion times vacuum `Realized(True)` and its white/reflective/specular `Realized(False)` follow the tier text of `bc-method-realizability`.
2. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` as to the transit exemption (the user's ruling, thirteenth exchange; replaced by decision 29); the carve-branch half stands. **The leaves are not additive with the suite green, and land on the carve branch.** `[M]` the qualname uniqueness gate (`tests/gates/values/test_content_identity_relocation.py:317`) and the content-identity roster gate (`tests/gates/values/test_content_identity.py:445`) both redden on a new `AlbedoBoundary`/`SpecularReturn`/`IsotropicReturn`/`ContentIdentity` class. Choice: a transit exemption with a live-edge witness (5.0), roster entries in each leaf, and integration of the leaves onto the carve branch, not `main`. Reason: the alternative, minting the colliding names at S2, would put the heart of the type system (the albedo) in a slice that also changes the SN consumers, defeating the gates-first discipline; the alternative of pushing the leaves to `main` would need the exemption on `main`. The orchestration plan's sentence "nothing consumes the leaves, so the suite stays green at every unit" is therefore true only with the exemption. This edits one existing shared gate file in L2: the orchestrator confirms.
3. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` as a leaf (L3 no longer exists; its gates are S2a's, decision 29). **L3 is the generic mechanism with a test-local table; each method's real table lands in its slice** (S2 to S4). Reason: attack elegance S4 (twin realizer bodies beside the old ladders for all of WF2.2); keeps L3 file-disjoint from every consumer.
4. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` by the user's ruling of the thirteenth exchange; replaced by decision 28 (the twin is one value, one digest). **Two digests, one realization, for `WallCondition.vacuum()` and `AlbedoBoundary(0.0, shape)`** (adopted point 4), with vacuum-ness answered from the realized `ZeroMap` type (adopted point 5), closing attack A1 without merging. Arithmetic collapses to the canonical form (a zero weight is dropped, `0 * x` is `WallCondition.vacuum()`), so only direct construction yields the twin. Trigger to merge: the #529 parameter chart declaring a canonical point at `alpha = 0`.
5. **The certification goes in L4 and folds onto `Quadrature.ordinate_permutation`** (as `ordinate_pairing(quadrature, motion)`, decision 35). Reason: the scaled ordinate permutation needs the certified permutation, and `[M]` `directional.py:455` already returns it; a second derivation beside the three `assert_*` re-derivations (today) is the twin the returned permutation removes.
6. `[CONFIRMED 2026-10-22, orchestrator C7]`, the exact-cover refusal as its witness (S2b gate 12). **`boundary_conditions` is keyword-only, trailing, default empty** (`BoundaryConditions.empty()`), against A's required parameter. Reason: the exact cover makes the empty default safe (a tagged carrier with no binding is refused naming each tag), a deck-only carrier (the PWR half cell) needs nothing, and a required parameter would force `{}` on 825 entry calls where only the tagged ones need it. `BoundaryConditions` differs from `Materials` in admitting empty, for that reason.
7. **The float rule's constant is promoted, not re-declared** (`chart._LEVEL_AGREEMENT` to `COORDINATE_AGREEMENT_ULPS`, 3 sites): X4, one convention. `values/scalars.py` stays tolerance-free.
8. **Combination (D2), the reading.** `[CONFIRMED 2026-10-22, orchestrator C7]` in outcome; `[REVISED 2026-10-22]` in mechanism (decision 28). One normalized combination, non-negative coefficients, total return at most 1 checked at construction, signed combinations kept as a separate value that cannot be bound. The mechanism is the algebra of the ONE type `WallCondition` (`+` and non-negative `*` add the network chain by chain and the source term by term, then the construction parse runs), with `WallDisplacement` the signed value; DRAFT 1's `CombinedBoundary`, its `normal_form(terms, cone=)` body and its collapse rules do not exist. `WallDisplacement` (renamed from `BoundaryDirection` by the homonym check of section 1.3) has 2 consumers today (the two negative-coefficient tests of `test_law_composition.py`) and #529; if the orchestrator prefers to delete signed values, those two rows are re-posed and the type goes (a size, not a principle).
9. `[REVISED 2026-10-22, orchestrator, DRAFT 3]` by decision 54 (`Wall` COMPOSES `WallAmplitudes`; the text below is DRAFT 2's, kept beside its marker). **`Wall` keeps its fields and delegates its bounds to `WallAmplitudes`** (design B composes). Reason: three test files build `Walls(Wall(...))` directly; one message owner (X4) is achieved without field churn.
10. `[CONFIRMED 2026-10-22, orchestrator C7]`. **Invariance of a declared profile is the declaration's, and conservative** (sharpening of ruling 2's phrase "only the method can see that"). `LabFrameInflow.invariant_under(h) = h.is_identity`; no sampling, no tolerance. Reason: attack elegance B5 (a sampled check keyed on nothing numerical) and attack math 4 (invariance is a declaration verb; equivariance is the method's, and `ordinate_pairing` certifies it). Reported as a letter-of-the-ruling tension, not resolved by overriding it: the check still sits beside the admission query (in `bind`).
11. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` by the user's ruling of the thirteenth exchange; replaced by decision 31 (a strict `xfail` naming #547, MC's cell `Refused`). **MC's cylinder (the open ruling of the measurement), PROVISIONAL.** Spelling: the cylinder's outer face is a tag bound to `WallCondition.specular(1.0)`, the same declaration MoC uses on the same cell, and MC's entry is `Approximated("a periodic equal-area square lattice cell (a torus wrap); the declared specular return is not what is computed", 547)`. Reason: R2 applied uniformly means MoC and MC on one cylinder cell take ONE `(material_mesh, boundary_conditions)` pair (the ninth exchange's purpose: two methods compared on the same pair), and `tests/gates/mc/test_gaps.py:692` compares MC on an `MCMesh` of a cylinder with the `ConcentricPinCell`. It is still false physics: MC computes a TRANSLATION of a square cell (a deck on a carrier that does not exist yet), not a specular return of a cylinder; design B's white is false in the same way. The `Approximated` name states what is computed, so the admission is honest about the departure even though the declaration is not. Principled alternative: MC takes a 2-D geometry value (the trigger named in the #514 guard); that is the user's to rule. The 3 positive sites (`test_gaps.py:692`, `test_properties.py:157`, `:205`) and the 3 refusal tests (`test_characteristic_walls.py:212-216`) follow it.
12. **The unregistered-type door derives the axis** (`state_cone` on the table, an Enum): a condition with `return_fraction < 0` on a cone-state table is `STATE_CONE`, otherwise `PLUMBING`. Reason: attack elegance S1's remedy; lets SN refuse `ZeroFluxBoundary` on the theory's axis without importing diffusion.
13. **The table key includes the face** (`FaceSite`), the measurement's finding (b): CP's forced left mirror, SN's hollow inner face; MC's period-equals-extent is a separate invariant (a guard, filed as #F3), not an admission.
14. `[CONFIRMED 2026-10-22, orchestrator C7]`. **The hollow inner face has a carrier.** `RadialAxisMesh.role_inner` (S1), outside `face_labels`; the binding carries it, `realize_boundary` admits every face and realizes only the trace's faces. Reason: D5 makes the 25 sites tags, and `RadialAxisMesh` had one slot (`bc_outer`), the legacy adapter synthesizing the inner mirror (`legacy_adapter.py:171`).
15. **Bare albedo in diffusion-only tests is `WallCondition.white(a)`** (R4). Reason: the new type has no shape field and diffusion annihilates the shape; white is the physical default of a diffuse exterior.
16. **`MaterialMesh` identity is keyed by roles; the method head's contractibility key adds the binding's conditions through `_law_key`'s existing try/except** (`hash`, else `(qualname, id)`). Reason: the laws are in `_axis_key` today (`material_mesh.py:96`), so two problems with the same mesh and different laws are different phase spaces, and the three open-sampler inflows (`_ManufacturedFaceInflow`, `_Liar`, `_Ramp`) have no content; `Binding` stays a `ContentIdentity` value (its digest raises `ContentlessError` for them) and the head does not call it. The alternative (`Binding` without the mixin, keyed by the head only) loses the roster gate's perturbation legs for no gain.
17. `[REFUTED 2026-10-22]` (L2 and L3 are no longer leaves; L1, L4 and L5 are all stage A: decision 29). **L4 moves to stage A, L5 stays in stage B, and L2's deck-operand gate is L3's.** Reason: L4 needs only `numerics` and `geometry.transformation`; L2 may not import L1 at runtime (its `FaceIsometry` annotations are `TYPE_CHECKING` and its gates use a stand-in with `is_identity`), and `0.5 * MirrorDeck()` needs L1, so that row lives in L3.
18. **`orpheus/sn/boundary/realizer.py` keeps its path** (design B names `realizers.py`): the helper functions, the docstring anchors of the theory page and `git blame` stay; the content is the table.
19. **Error roots:** `BoundaryError` in `transport/boundary_conditions/errors.py` (the eleventh exchange: geometry holds no module named for boundary conditions), `DeckError` in geometry, `OrdinatePairingError` in numerics. Reason: each in the lowest layer whose vocabulary defines it; the census's "rooted in geometry" memo predates the eleventh exchange.
20. **Faces are plain `str`, no `FaceName` type** (design A assumed WP-B would mint one): `[M]` `orpheus/geometry/faces.py` exports `face_name`, `face_normal`, `face_opposite`, `AXIS_NAMES`, `AXIS_INDEX`, `FACE_NAMES` and no `FaceName`. The strongest claim is the tree.
21. **`GeometrySpecification.exterior`** is a trailing field defaulting to empty, with exact cover; the name `exterior` and the type `WallAmplitudes` are design B's NEEDS item 2 and are taken.
22. **The count of meaning-change sites** is 100 reflective plus 8 periodic by `_curved_sites.py` (six constructor arms by literal coordinate system) against design B's 101 plus 8 (it counted 8 `Mesh1D(face_laws=...)` sites where the coordinate system is a variable, which an AST pass over literals cannot decide; my K5 arm found 0). The 25 of D5 are recovered exactly (23 inner reflective + 2 inner periodic). The re-count is owed at S1's tip, where the first red enumerates the population: after S1's geometry refuses a deck on a curved face, every remaining site reddens at construction.
23. **Slices own files by the latest slice among the packages a file imports** (`_slice_assign.py`), because that is the first slice after which the file can be green; the rule over-assigns nothing to S5 beyond the 36 files that import a boundary-naming derivations module or the specification.
24. **Rule 4 filings.** `[DONE 2026-10-22]` F1, F2, F3 are #653, #654, #655, and diffusion's source refusal is #656 (`gh issue view`, all OPEN); the admission pins quote them (decision 41). Not filed because the carve fixes them in-session: CP's `alpha` drop (now a refusal), SN curvilinear's construct-then-refuse (now refused at `admit`), the incidental `ValueError("Unknown face 'xmin'")` for a periodic on a curved face (now a `DeckError` at the geometry). The diffusion super-Markov acceptance is #265's albedo item only (remedied; the issue stays open). `process-discipline`: a finding fixed this session is not filed.
25. **The `BoundaryCondition` alias rename** (`ReferenceBoundaryKind`, with the field `ProblemSpec.boundary_conditions` renamed `boundary_kinds`, C10) lands in S5 with the alias gaining the `"zero_flux"` member it already carries in two cases (`cases/diffusion.py:749,924`, a latent type lie).
26. **`CLAUDE.md`'s L0 row ("may import `numerics/`, `geometry/`, `mesh/`, `data/`") omits `specification`**, while 2 derivations modules import it and the gate allows it: a present-tense-false row owed to its source page (`docs/development/onboarding.md`, `CLAUDE.md` is generated), handed to the archivist at WF2.5.
27. **The surgical-carve exception is recalibrated for this trial** (`workflow_orchestration.md`, "Ruled" 1): the leaf and slice units are Sonnet units with gates first and Fable reviews. Nothing in this specification depends on that choice; the briefs are written for an executor with no session context.

**Decisions of the amendment (DRAFT 2, 2026-10-22 night).** Each carries its source and its marker. They apply the thirteenth exchange of `boundary_law_ontology.md` (A, B) and the orchestrator's decisions C1 to C7 of the WF2.1 amendment brief, after the two checks `check_elegance.md` and `check_structure.md`.

28. **One shared exterior type, whose linear part is a tensor network (A; the user, thirteenth exchange).** `WallCondition(network: ResponseNetwork, inflow: InflowTerms)` (DRAFT 2 spelled the field `source`, which does not construct: decision 47); `R = s P + d (E o Rt)`; vacuum, white, specular and inflow are classmethod facades (D1); `AlbedoBoundary`, `VacuumBoundary`, `InflowBoundary`, `CombinedBoundary`, `SpecularReturn`, `IsotropicReturn`, `ReturnShape`, `UniformInflow` and the kernel tier do not exist as new types; the twin `WallCondition.vacuum()` / `AlbedoBoundary(0, .)` is gone (one value, one digest). The closed sums are the chains (`SpecularChain`, `DiffuseChain`) over the typed factors (`MirrorPermutation`, `CurrentRetraction`, `IsotropicSection`) with a decidable composition parse; the zero network and the source are the other keys of the method's table. Method-only responses (`ZeroFluxCondition`, an anisotropic SN kernel) stay their own types. Realization is a functor on the network: the typed realized operators are the images of `0`, `s P`, `d (E o Rt)` and the image of a sum is the sum of the images (law L-L). `[R]` the structure of the type follows the ruling's words; the minimal closed vocabulary (two chains, three factors, one marker) is the orchestrator-reviewable part: a larger tensor-network hierarchy in `transport` was not minted.
29. **The migration premise is reversed (C3; the user, thirteenth exchange).** No transit exemption on the qualname gate; the conditions package (DRAFT 1's L2 and L3) moves in the sequential carve as a move, in S2 ("S2a"), gates first; the only additive leaves are L1, L4 and L5, with L5 reduced to `WallAmplitudes` (and, at DRAFT 3, L4 reduced to the certification and the `TraceRole` widening: decision 45). `[M]` `_amend_names.py`: none of the 33 names of the three leaves is defined in `orpheus/` or `tests/` today (positive control found). Refutes DRAFT 1's decisions 2 (as to the exemption; the carve-branch half stands), 3 and 17.
30. **The names, with their homonym checks (`_amend_names.py`: AST over 1148 tracked Python files, 0 definitions each; positive control `BoundaryTraceLaw` found once).** `WallCondition` (the reference's `Wall` is a positioned wall of the reference, a different identifier; the strongest claim on "wall" stays with the reference, and `WallAmplitudes(specular, diffuse)` is the coordinate pair of the linear part); `ResponseNetwork`, `Rung` (withdrawn at DRAFT 3: decision 49), `MirrorPermutation`, `CurrentRetraction`, `IsotropicSection`, `SpecularChain`, `DiffuseChain`, `ZeroNetwork` (the factor names say the arrow: retraction and section are `axis_marginal.py`'s words, "embedding" is not an operator name); `InflowTerms`; `WallDisplacement` (renamed from `BoundaryDirection`: "direction" is `Omega` in 57 files of `orpheus/`, so the draft's name reads as an ordinate; a displacement in the affine hull of the cone is what the value is); `StateCone` (an Enum, replacing the `signed_trace` bool); `ZeroFluxCondition` (renamed from `ZeroFluxBoundary` so that no new class shares a qualname with a retiring one: the old class is named by 18 files across slices S2, S3 and S5 and cannot die before WF2.4, and the `Condition` suffix of the two condition types separates them from the `Boundary` classes of the old tree; every other new name is fresh by the AST pass). ## 11. The WF2.2 record (the leaves integrated, 2026-10-23)

`[M]` workflow `wf_cfc7e8c5-7cd`, 18 agents, about 3.1 M sub-agent tokens, 78 min. Per unit: the Sonnet test-architect wrote the gates red at the base tip, the Sonnet implementer built, Opus qa and the elegance-enforcer reviewed in parallel, a Sonnet amender answered, and an Opus verifier checked every disposition with in-process mutations. Integrated on `refactor/wave2-leaves` from `d9d638e6`: L1 by fast-forward, L4 and L5 by cherry-pick, without conflicts.

What the reviews caught before integration, each verified red-then-green by a mutation:
- L1: the colouring gates did not pin the mirror's position (offsets 0 and `total` were green); `ends` was a second spelling of the first and last breakpoint, so an inconsistent pair made an asymmetric slab symmetric; the partition was not parsed (unsorted breaks and zero-width intervals were admitted); `FacePairing` checked only the linear part of its motion, so a face glued to itself by a translation or by the identity constructed.
- L4: the folded-rule diagnosis of `_specular.py` (#434 R2) was dropped, so a σ-folded rule was misreported as a wrong pairing (ERR-045); widening `TraceRole` widened `AngularFaceTraceSpace.role` to the angle-integrated tier (now `OrdinateTraceRole`, composed into `TraceRole`); the two shape guards of the axis reading hid each other.
- L5: the bounds refusal dropped the pinned fragment "not a physical wall"; the owned-once gates counted only the combined-wall fragment; the scope boundary carried no `SCOPE-BOUNDARY[guard]` token.

Rulings of the orchestrator at integration:
1. A `FacePairing` exists only for a minted deck: a distinct-face pair whose motion is not a translation is refused, a declared scope boundary whose revisit is the first minted deferred deck (`f6690aeb`). The precedent row (a quarter turn between opposite faces) was wrong geometry: a rotation glues adjacent faces.
2. `orpheus/numerics/tangential.py`, outside L4's file set, is accepted (section 2.5).
3. The riders above: S1's two delegations and the `test_deck.py` row; S2b's fixtures, the `OrdinatePairing` decision and the docstring line; S5's re-baseline R-8b. Follow-ups outside the wave: #657 (a `RigidMotion` reads its own reflection plane) and a comment on #436 (the translation's length is the fundamental domain's period).
4. `deck`, `colouring` and `exterior` are rendered on their API pages now; `ordinate_pairing` waits for WF2.5 with the quadrature package, which no API page renders.
